Create `Tests/CreatorOCCTTests/Support/MeshSolidFixtures.swift`:

```swift
// Test fixture file: closed triangle meshes for Mesh to Solid on OCCT. CreatorOCCTTests cannot see CreatorImplicit.
import CreatorGeometry

enum MeshSolidFixtures {
    struct Mesh {
        var positions: [SIMD3<Float>]
        var triangles: [UInt32]
    }

    /// A cube from `corner` of the given `size`, twelve triangles, counter-clockwise seen from outside (or from inside when `inside`).
    static func cube(size: Float, at corner: SIMD3<Float> = .zero, inside: Bool = false) -> Mesh {
        let positions = (0..<8).map { bit in
            corner + size * SIMD3<Float>(bit & 1 == 0 ? 0 : 1, bit & 2 == 0 ? 0 : 1, bit & 4 == 0 ? 0 : 1)
        }
        // Vertex numbers are bits (x, y, z); each face is two triangles.
        let outward: [UInt32] = [0, 2, 3, 0, 3, 1, 4, 5, 7, 4, 7, 6, 0, 1, 5, 0, 5, 4, 2, 6, 7, 2, 7, 3, 0, 4, 6, 0, 6, 2, 1, 3, 7, 1, 7, 5]
        var triangles = outward
        if inside {
            for base in stride(from: 0, to: triangles.count, by: 3) { triangles.swapAt(base + 1, base + 2) }
        }
        return Mesh(positions: positions, triangles: triangles)
    }

    /// A sphere of `radius` from an octahedron split `subdivisions` times; 8 × 4^n triangles.
    static func sphere(radius: Double, subdivisions: Int) -> Mesh {
        var points = [Vector3(1, 0, 0), Vector3(-1, 0, 0), Vector3(0, 1, 0), Vector3(0, -1, 0), Vector3(0, 0, 1), Vector3(0, 0, -1)]
        var faces: [[Int]] = [[0, 2, 4], [2, 1, 4], [1, 3, 4], [3, 0, 4], [2, 0, 5], [1, 2, 5], [3, 1, 5], [0, 3, 5]]
        for _ in 0..<subdivisions {
            var middles: [[Int]: Int] = [:]
            func middle(_ a: Int, _ b: Int) -> Int {
                let key = [min(a, b), max(a, b)]
                if let known = middles[key] { return known }
                points.append(((points[a] + points[b]) * 0.5).normalized ?? .unitX)
                middles[key] = points.count - 1
                return points.count - 1
            }
            faces = faces.flatMap { face -> [[Int]] in
                let (a, b, c) = (face[0], face[1], face[2])
                let (ab, bc, ca) = (middle(a, b), middle(b, c), middle(c, a))
                return [[a, ab, ca], [ab, b, bc], [ca, bc, c], [ab, bc, ca]]
            }
        }
        let positions = points.map { SIMD3(Float($0.x * radius), Float($0.y * radius), Float($0.z * radius)) }
        return Mesh(positions: positions, triangles: faces.flatMap { $0.map { UInt32($0) } })
    }

    /// The enclosed volume of a mesh in cubic millimetres.
    static func volume(_ mesh: Mesh) -> Double {
        func point(_ index: UInt32) -> Vector3 {
            let p = mesh.positions[Int(index)]
            return Vector3(Double(p.x), Double(p.y), Double(p.z))
        }
        var sum = 0.0
        for base in stride(from: 0, to: mesh.triangles.count, by: 3) {
            sum += point(mesh.triangles[base]).dot(point(mesh.triangles[base + 1]).cross(point(mesh.triangles[base + 2])))
        }
        return sum / 6
    }

    /// `first` and `second` as one mesh.
    static func joined(_ first: Mesh, _ second: Mesh) -> Mesh {
        Mesh(positions: first.positions + second.positions,
             triangles: first.triangles + second.triangles.map { $0 + UInt32(first.positions.count) })
    }
}
```

Create `Tests/CreatorOCCTTests/MeshToSolidTests.swift`:

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorKernel
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import CreatorOCCT

/// Mesh to Solid on OpenCascade (implicit spec §4.7, §7): valid, the mesh's volume, STEP-exportable, faces tagged by the operation.
struct MeshToSolidTests {
    let kernel = OCCTKernel()

    func solid(_ mesh: MeshSolidFixtures.Mesh, tag: NodeTag = newTag()) async throws -> Solid {
        try await kernel.meshToSolid(positions: mesh.positions, triangles: mesh.triangles, tag: tag)
    }

    func isValid(_ solid: Solid) async throws -> Bool {
        let shape = try await kernel.shape(of: solid)
        return OCCTKernel.serialized { shape.isValid }
    }

    @Test func aCubeOfTwelveTrianglesIsASolidOfSixFaces() async throws {
        let cube = MeshSolidFixtures.cube(size: 10)
        let made = try await solid(cube)
        let valid = try await isValid(made)
        #expect(made.topology.faces.count == 6, "the coplanar triangles are merged")
        #expect(valid)
        let properties = try await kernel.properties(of: made)
        #expect(abs(properties.volume - 1000) < 1e-6 && abs(properties.surfaceArea - 600) < 1e-6)
        #expect(abs(made.bounds.size.x - 10) < 1e-9 && abs(made.bounds.max.z - 10) < 1e-9)
    }

    @Test func aSphereKeepsEveryFacetAndTheMeshsVolume() async throws {
        let sphere = MeshSolidFixtures.sphere(radius: 5, subdivisions: 3)
        let made = try await solid(sphere)
        let valid = try await isValid(made)
        #expect(made.topology.faces.count == 512 && valid)
        let volume = try await kernel.properties(of: made).volume
        let expected = MeshSolidFixtures.volume(sphere)
        #expect(abs(volume - expected) / expected < 1e-6, "\(volume) against \(expected)")
    }

    @Test func aMeshWithACavityIsASolidWithAVoid() async throws {
        let cavity = MeshSolidFixtures.cube(size: 6, at: SIMD3(2, 2, 2), inside: true)
        let part = MeshSolidFixtures.joined(MeshSolidFixtures.cube(size: 10), cavity)
        let made = try await solid(part)
        let (valid, volume) = (try await isValid(made), try await kernel.properties(of: made).volume)
        #expect(made.topology.faces.count == 12 && valid)
        #expect(abs(volume - (1000 - 216)) < 1e-6)
    }

    @Test func twoSeparateCubesAreTwoSolids() async throws {
        let pair = MeshSolidFixtures.joined(MeshSolidFixtures.cube(size: 10), MeshSolidFixtures.cube(size: 4, at: SIMD3(20, 0, 0)))
        let made = try await solid(pair)
        let (valid, volume) = (try await isValid(made), try await kernel.properties(of: made).volume)
        #expect(made.topology.faces.count == 12 && valid)
        #expect(abs(volume - (1000 + 64)) < 1e-6)
    }

    @Test func everyFaceCarriesTheOperationsTag() async throws {
        let tag = newTag(item: 3)
        let made = try await solid(MeshSolidFixtures.cube(size: 10), tag: tag)
        #expect(made.topology.faces.allSatisfy { face in face.tags.contains { $0.node == tag.node && $0.item == 3 } })
        // A face is told from the others by its normal, as any solid's is.
        let top = made.topology.faces.filter { ($0.normal ?? .zero).dot(.unitZ) > 0.99 }
        #expect(top.count == 1)
    }

    @Test func theSolidExportsAsStepAndReadsBack() async throws {
        let made = try await solid(MeshSolidFixtures.sphere(radius: 5, subdivisions: 2))
        let url = URL.temporaryDirectory.appending(path: "mesh-to-solid-\(UUID().uuidString).step")
        defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) }
        try await kernel.export([made], format: .step, to: url)
        let size = (try? FileManager.default.attributesOfItem(atPath: url.path)[.size] as? Int) ?? 0
        #expect(size > 1_000)
        let back = try OCCTKernel.serialized { try OCCTShape.readSTEP(url) }
        let expected = try await kernel.properties(of: made).volume
        let readBack = OCCTKernel.serialized { back.volume }
        #expect(abs(readBack - expected) / expected < 1e-6)
    }

    @Test func aMeshThatIsNotClosedSaysSo() async throws {
        var open = MeshSolidFixtures.cube(size: 10)
        open.triangles.removeLast(3)
        let error = await #expect(throws: KernelError.self) { try await solid(open) }
        #expect(error?.userMessage.contains("not closed") == true, "\(String(describing: error))")
    }

    @Test func aMeshWoundInsideOutSaysSo() async throws {
        let error = await #expect(throws: KernelError.self) { try await solid(MeshSolidFixtures.cube(size: 10, inside: true)) }
        #expect(error?.userMessage.contains("inside out") == true, "\(String(describing: error))")
    }

    @Test func tooFewTrianglesAndBadIndicesAreRefused() async {
        await #expect(throws: KernelError.invalidInput("A mesh needs at least four triangles to make a solid.")) {
            try await kernel.meshToSolid(positions: [.zero], triangles: [0, 0, 0], tag: newTag())
        }
        let broken = MeshSolidFixtures.Mesh(positions: MeshSolidFixtures.cube(size: 1).positions,
                                            triangles: MeshSolidFixtures.cube(size: 1).triangles.dropLast() + [99])
        let error = await #expect(throws: KernelError.self) { try await solid(broken) }
        #expect(error?.userMessage.contains("names a vertex it does not have") == true)
    }
}
```

```swift
import Testing
@testable import CreatorImplicit

/// Merging vertices that sit on one another, before a mesh becomes a solid.
struct MeshWelderTests {
    @Test func aMeshWithNothingToMergeKeepsItsShape() {
        let cube = MeshFixtures.cube
        let welded = MeshWelder.weld(positions: cube.positions, triangles: cube.triangles)
        let result = TriangleMesh(positions: welded.positions, triangles: welded.triangles)
        #expect(result.vertexCount == 8 && result.triangleCount == 12 && abs(result.signedVolume - 1) < 1e-9)
        #expect(MeshTopology(result).isClosedManifold)
    }

    @Test func aCubeWithItsVerticesDoubledPerFaceIsOneClosedCube() {
        let soup = MeshFixtures.cubeSoup
        let positions = soup.positions.map { SIMD3(Float($0.x), Float($0.y), Float($0.z)) }
        let welded = MeshWelder.weld(positions: positions, triangles: soup.indices)
        #expect(welded.positions.count == 8 && welded.triangles.count == 36)
        #expect(MeshTopology(TriangleMesh(positions: welded.positions, triangles: welded.triangles)).isClosedManifold)
    }

    @Test func aSliverCollapsedByTheWeldTakesItsTriangleAndKeepsTheMeshClosed() {
        // The sphere with one vertex split off by a hair: the two are one again, and nothing is left open.
        let sphere = MeshFixtures.sphereMesh(radius: 5, subdivisions: 2)
        var positions = sphere.positions
        var triangles = sphere.triangles
        positions.append(positions[0] + SIMD3(1e-8, 0, 0))
        let copy = UInt32(positions.count - 1)
        if let first = triangles.firstIndex(of: 0) { triangles[first] = copy }
        let welded = MeshWelder.weld(positions: positions, triangles: triangles)
        let result = TriangleMesh(positions: welded.positions, triangles: welded.triangles)
        #expect(result.vertexCount == sphere.vertexCount && result.triangleCount == sphere.triangleCount)
        #expect(MeshTopology(result).isClosedManifold)
    }

    @Test func aTriangleThatCollapsesIsDroppedAndUnusedVerticesGo() {
        let positions: [SIMD3<Float>] = [SIMD3(0, 0, 0), SIMD3(1, 0, 0), SIMD3(0, 1, 0), SIMD3(1e-9, 0, 0), SIMD3(5, 5, 5)]
        let welded = MeshWelder.weld(positions: positions, triangles: [0, 1, 2, 0, 3, 1, 0, 1, 99])
        #expect(welded.triangles == [0, 1, 2] && welded.positions.count == 3)
    }
}
```

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import CreatorKernel
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import CreatorNodes
#if canImport(CreatorOCCT)
@testable import CreatorOCCT
#endif

/// Mesh to Solid (implicit spec §3.6): the cap in the spec's words, and, on OpenCascade, the solid.
struct MeshToSolidNodeTests {
    func solidOfASphere(_ h: inout Harness) -> (mesh: Node, solid: Node) {
        let sphere = h.add(ImplicitSphereNode.self, ["radius": .number(3)])
        let mesh = h.add(MeshNode.self, ["preview resolution": .number(0.4)])
        let solid = h.add(MeshToSolidNode.self)
        h.wire(sphere, "implicit", to: mesh, "implicit")
        h.wire(mesh, "mesh", to: solid, "mesh")
        return (mesh, solid)
    }

    @Test func aMeshAboveTheLimitIsRefusedInTheSpecsWords() {
        #expect(throws: Never.self) { try MeshToSolidNode.requireWithinLimit(100_000) }
        #expect(throws: NodeError.invalidValue("Mesh has 100,001 triangles; Mesh to Solid takes at most 100,000. "
            + "Decimate it first or coarsen the resolution.")) { try MeshToSolidNode.requireWithinLimit(100_001) }
        #expect(throws: NodeError.invalidValue("Mesh has 2,000,000 triangles; Mesh to Solid takes at most 100,000. "
            + "Decimate it first or coarsen the resolution.")) { try MeshToSolidNode.requireWithinLimit(2_000_000) }
    }

    @Test func aKernelWithNoBrepSaysSo() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let solid = solidOfASphere(&h).solid
        let fake = try await h.run([solid])
        #expect(fake.error(solid) == "Mesh to solid isn't supported by this kernel yet.")
        let none = try await h.run([solid], kernel: KernelUnavailable())
        #expect(none.error(solid) == "Geometry isn't available on this platform yet.")
    }

    #if canImport(CreatorOCCT)
    @Test func aMeshedBallBecomesAFacetedSolidOfTheSameVolumeThatExportsAsStep() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let (meshNode, solidNode) = solidOfASphere(&h)
        let output = h.add(OutputNode.self)
        h.wire(solidNode, "solid", to: output, "solid")
        let kernel = OCCTKernel()
        let report = try await h.run([output], kernel: kernel)
        #expect(report.isOK(solidNode), "\(String(describing: report.state(solidNode)))")
        let made = try onlySolid(report, solidNode)
        let mesh = try #require(report.triangleMesh(meshNode))
        #expect(made.topology.faces.count > 1_000 && made.topology.faces.count <= mesh.triangleCount, "facets in one plane are merged")
        let found = try await volume(made, kernel)
        #expect(abs(found - mesh.signedVolume) / mesh.signedVolume < 1e-6, "\(found) against \(mesh.signedVolume)")
        let url = URL.temporaryDirectory.appending(path: "mesh-to-solid-node-\(UUID().uuidString).step")
        defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) }
        try await kernel.export([made], format: .step, to: url)
        #expect(((try? FileManager.default.attributesOfItem(atPath: url.path)[.size] as? Int) ?? 0) > 1_000)
    }

    /// A decimated mesh's neighbouring planes differ by float noise, so the shim (vertices and edges shared by index, only exactly
    /// coplanar neighbours unified, no `BRepBuilderAPI_Sewing`) merges fewer faces than it does for an exact mesh. Fewer merged faces is
    /// expected; an invalid solid or a lost volume is not.
    @Test(arguments: ["ball", "bracket"])
    func aDecimatedMeshBecomesAValidSolidOfTheMeshsVolumeWithFewerFacesThanTheMeshHadTriangles(_ part: String) async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let shape: Node
        if part == "ball" {
            shape = h.add(ImplicitSphereNode.self, ["radius": .number(5)])
        } else {
            let plate = h.add(ImplicitBoxNode.self, ["size": .vector(Vector3(20, 14, 5))])
            let hole = h.add(ImplicitCylinderNode.self, ["radius": .number(2), "height": .number(8)])
            shape = h.add(ImplicitSubtractNode.self)
            h.wire(plate, "implicit", to: shape, "shapes")
            h.wire(hole, "implicit", to: shape, "shapes")
        }
        let mesh = h.add(MeshNode.self, ["preview resolution": .number(0.5)])
        let decimate = h.add(DecimateNode.self, ["tolerance": .number(0.05)])
        let solid = h.add(MeshToSolidNode.self)
        let output = h.add(OutputNode.self)
        h.wire(shape, "implicit", to: mesh, "implicit")
        h.wire(mesh, "mesh", to: decimate, "mesh")
        h.wire(decimate, "mesh", to: solid, "mesh")
        h.wire(solid, "solid", to: output, "solid")
        let kernel = OCCTKernel()
        let report = try await h.run([output], kernel: kernel)
        #expect(report.isOK(solid), "\(String(describing: report.state(solid)))")
        let before = try #require(report.triangleMesh(mesh)), after = try #require(report.triangleMesh(decimate))
        let made = try onlySolid(report, solid)
        let shapeOfSolid = try await kernel.shape(of: made)
        #expect(OCCTKernel.serialized { shapeOfSolid.isValid })
        let faces = made.topology.faces.count
        #expect(faces >= 4 && faces <= after.triangleCount && after.triangleCount < before.triangleCount / 2,
                "\(before.triangleCount) → \(after.triangleCount) triangles → \(faces) faces")
        if part == "ball" {
            #expect(faces == after.triangleCount, "no two facets of a curved surface are coplanar, so none merge")
        } else {
            #expect(faces < after.triangleCount && faces >= 6, "the flat sides merge into few faces, but not always into six")
        }
        let found = try await volume(made, kernel)
        #expect(abs(found - after.signedVolume) / after.signedVolume < 1e-6, "\(found) against \(after.signedVolume)")
    }
    #endif
}
```

```c
/// A faceted solid from a closed triangle mesh (implicit spec §4.7): `positions` is `vertex_count` x, y, z triples in millimetres and
/// `triangles` three vertex numbers per face, counter-clockwise seen from outside. A planar face is built per triangle, the faces
/// are sewn (BRepBuilderAPI_Sewing), same-domain faces are merged (ShapeUpgrade_UnifySameDomain), a shell facing inwards becomes a
/// void of the solid that contains it, and BRepCheck_Analyzer must accept the result. A mesh that is not closed, not a manifold, wound
/// inside out or rejected by the checker fails with the reason in `status`. Several outer surfaces give a compound of solids.
occt_shape *occt_mesh_to_solid(const double *positions, int vertex_count, const unsigned int *triangles, int triangle_count,
                               occt_status *status);
```

Create `Sources/COCCT/cocct_mesh_solid.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "cocct_internal.hpp"

#include <BRepBndLib.hxx>
#include <BRepBuilderAPI_MakeEdge.hxx>
#include <BRepBuilderAPI_MakeFace.hxx>
#include <BRepBuilderAPI_MakeSolid.hxx>
#include <BRepCheck_Analyzer.hxx>
#include <BRep_Builder.hxx>
#include <Bnd_Box.hxx>
#include <ShapeUpgrade_UnifySameDomain.hxx>
#include <TopoDS_Compound.hxx>
#include <TopoDS_Edge.hxx>
#include <TopoDS_Shell.hxx>
#include <TopoDS_Solid.hxx>
#include <TopoDS_Vertex.hxx>
#include <TopoDS_Wire.hxx>
#include <gp_Pnt.hxx>
#include <gp_Vec.hxx>

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <numeric>
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>

using namespace cocct;

namespace {

/// One undirected edge of the mesh: the shared OCCT edge, and how the triangles use it.
struct mesh_edge {
    TopoDS_Edge edge;
    int uses = 0;
    int forward_uses = 0;
    int first_triangle = -1;
};

/// A set of triangles joined by shared edges: one closed surface of the mesh.
struct component {
    TopoDS_Shell shell;
    Bnd_Box box;
    double volume = 0;
};

/// Union-find over triangle numbers.
struct triangle_groups {
    std::vector<int> parent;

    explicit triangle_groups(int count) : parent(static_cast<size_t>(count)) { std::iota(parent.begin(), parent.end(), 0); }

    int find(int i) {
        while (parent[i] != i) {
            parent[i] = parent[parent[i]];
            i = parent[i];
        }
        return i;
    }

    void join(int a, int b) { parent[find(a)] = find(b); }
};

bool box_contains(const Bnd_Box &outer, const Bnd_Box &inner) {
    double ox0, oy0, oz0, ox1, oy1, oz1, ix0, iy0, iz0, ix1, iy1, iz1;
    outer.Get(ox0, oy0, oz0, ox1, oy1, oz1);
    inner.Get(ix0, iy0, iz0, ix1, iy1, iz1);
    return ox0 <= ix0 && oy0 <= iy0 && oz0 <= iz0 && ox1 >= ix1 && oy1 >= iy1 && oz1 >= iz1;
}

/// Whether the triangle `other` lies in the plane of the triangle `base` (unit normal `normal`), as exactly as UnifySameDomain, whose
/// default tolerances are 1e-7 mm and 1e-12 rad, can tell: a mesh stored in single precision is rarely that flat, and then there is
/// nothing for the merge to do.
bool is_coplanar(const std::vector<gp_Pnt> &points, const unsigned int *base, const gp_Vec &normal, const unsigned int *other) {
    for (int k = 0; k < 3; ++k) {
        if (std::abs(normal.Dot(gp_Vec(points[base[0]], points[other[k]]))) > 1e-9) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

uint64_t edge_key(unsigned a, unsigned b) { return static_cast<uint64_t>(std::min(a, b)) << 32 | std::max(a, b); }

/// One solid per outward-facing surface, with each inward-facing one (a void) given to the smallest solid whose box holds it.
TopoDS_Shape solids_from(std::vector<component> &surfaces) {
    BRep_Builder builder;
    std::vector<TopoDS_Solid> solids;
    std::vector<size_t> outer;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < surfaces.size(); ++i) {
        if (surfaces[i].volume > 0) {
            BRepBuilderAPI_MakeSolid maker(surfaces[i].shell);
            solids.push_back(maker.Solid());
            outer.push_back(i);
        }
    }
    if (solids.empty()) {
        throw user_error("the mesh is wound inside out: its triangles must run counter-clockwise seen from outside");
    }
    for (const component &hollow : surfaces) {
        if (hollow.volume > 0) {
            continue;
        }
        int best = -1;
        for (size_t k = 0; k < outer.size(); ++k) {
            const component &candidate = surfaces[outer[k]];
            if (box_contains(candidate.box, hollow.box) && (best < 0 || candidate.volume < surfaces[outer[best]].volume)) {
                best = static_cast<int>(k);
            }
        }
        if (best < 0) {
            throw user_error("the mesh has a cavity that no outer surface contains");
        }
        builder.Add(solids[best], hollow.shell);
    }
    if (solids.size() == 1) {
        return solids[0];
    }
    TopoDS_Compound compound;
    builder.MakeCompound(compound);
    for (const TopoDS_Solid &solid : solids) {
        builder.Add(compound, solid);
    }
    return compound;
}

} // namespace

extern "C" {

occt_shape *occt_mesh_to_solid(const double *positions, int vertex_count, const unsigned int *triangles, int triangle_count,
                               occt_status *status) {
    return guarded(status, [&]() -> occt_shape * {
        if (!positions || !triangles || vertex_count < 4 || triangle_count < 4) {
            throw user_error("a mesh needs at least four triangles to make a solid");
        }
        // The vertices, made once: a triangle that names a vertex uses that vertex, so the surface is sewn by construction.
        BRep_Builder builder;
        std::vector<gp_Pnt> points;
        points.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(vertex_count));
        std::vector<TopoDS_Vertex> vertices(static_cast<size_t>(vertex_count));
        for (int i = 0; i < vertex_count; ++i) {
            const double *p = positions + 3 * i;
            if (!(std::isfinite(p[0]) && std::isfinite(p[1]) && std::isfinite(p[2]))) {
                throw user_error("the mesh has a vertex that is not a finite position");
            }
            points.emplace_back(p[0], p[1], p[2]);
            builder.MakeVertex(vertices[static_cast<size_t>(i)], points.back(), 1e-7);
        }

        // The edges, made once each, and the faces on them; the shell of a surface is its faces.
        std::unordered_map<uint64_t, mesh_edge> edges;
        edges.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(triangle_count) * 3 / 2 + 16);
        std::vector<TopoDS_Face> faces(static_cast<size_t>(triangle_count));
        std::vector<gp_Vec> normals(static_cast<size_t>(triangle_count));
        triangle_groups groups(triangle_count);
        bool has_coplanar_neighbours = false;
        for (int t = 0; t < triangle_count; ++t) {
            const unsigned int *corner = triangles + 3 * t;
            for (int k = 0; k < 3; ++k) {
                if (corner[k] >= static_cast<unsigned>(vertex_count)) {
                    throw user_error("the mesh has a triangle that names a vertex it does not have");
                }
            }
            if (corner[0] == corner[1] || corner[1] == corner[2] || corner[0] == corner[2]) {
                throw user_error("the mesh has a triangle with a repeated vertex (" + std::to_string(t) + ")");
            }
            const gp_Vec normal = gp_Vec(points[corner[0]], points[corner[1]]).Crossed(gp_Vec(points[corner[0]], points[corner[2]]));
            if (normal.Magnitude() < 1e-14) {
                throw user_error("the mesh has a triangle with no area (" + std::to_string(t) + ")");
            }
            normals[static_cast<size_t>(t)] = normal.Normalized();
            TopoDS_Wire wire;
            builder.MakeWire(wire);
            for (int side = 0; side < 3; ++side) {
                const unsigned from = corner[side], to = corner[(side + 1) % 3];
                mesh_edge &record = edges[edge_key(from, to)];
                if (record.uses == 0) {
                    BRepBuilderAPI_MakeEdge maker(vertices[std::min(from, to)], vertices[std::max(from, to)]);
                    if (!maker.IsDone()) {
                        throw user_error("the mesh has an edge too short for OpenCascade (triangle " + std::to_string(t) + ")");
                    }
                    record.edge = maker.Edge();
                    record.first_triangle = t;
                } else {
                    groups.join(record.first_triangle, t);
                    if (!has_coplanar_neighbours) {
                        has_coplanar_neighbours = is_coplanar(points, triangles + 3 * record.first_triangle, normals[static_cast<size_t>(record.first_triangle)], corner);
                    }
                }
                ++record.uses;
                const bool forward = from < to;
                if (forward) {
                    ++record.forward_uses;
                }
                builder.Add(wire, forward ? record.edge : TopoDS::Edge(record.edge.Reversed()));
            }
            wire.Closed(Standard_True);
            BRepBuilderAPI_MakeFace maker(wire, Standard_True);
            if (!maker.IsDone()) {
                throw user_error("the mesh has a triangle OpenCascade cannot make a face of (" + std::to_string(t) + ")");
            }
            faces[static_cast<size_t>(t)] = maker.Face();
        }
        int open = 0, shared_too_much = 0, mismatched = 0;
        for (const auto &entry : edges) {
            const mesh_edge &record = entry.second;
            if (record.uses == 1) {
                ++open;
            } else if (record.uses > 2) {
                ++shared_too_much;
            } else if (record.forward_uses != 1) {
                ++mismatched;
            }
        }
        if (open > 0) {
            throw user_error("the mesh is not closed: " + std::to_string(open) + " edges belong to a single triangle");
        }
        if (shared_too_much > 0) {
            throw user_error("the mesh is not a manifold: " + std::to_string(shared_too_much) +
                             " edges are shared by more than two triangles");
        }
        if (mismatched > 0) {
            throw user_error("the mesh is not consistently wound: " + std::to_string(mismatched) +
                             " edges are run the same way by both triangles that share them");
        }

        // The surfaces: the triangles joined by edges, each a shell with its volume (positive when it faces outwards).
        std::unordered_map<int, size_t> index_of;
        std::vector<component> surfaces;
        for (int t = 0; t < triangle_count; ++t) {
            const int root = groups.find(t);
            auto found = index_of.find(root);
            if (found == index_of.end()) {
                found = index_of.emplace(root, surfaces.size()).first;
                surfaces.emplace_back();
                builder.MakeShell(surfaces.back().shell);
            }
            component &surface = surfaces[found->second];
            builder.Add(surface.shell, faces[static_cast<size_t>(t)]);
            const unsigned int *corner = triangles + 3 * t;
            const gp_Pnt &a = points[corner[0]], &b = points[corner[1]], &c = points[corner[2]];
            surface.volume += gp_Vec(a.XYZ()).Dot(gp_Vec(b.XYZ()).Crossed(gp_Vec(c.XYZ()))) / 6;
            surface.box.Add(a);
            surface.box.Add(b);
            surface.box.Add(c);
        }
        for (component &surface : surfaces) {
            surface.shell.Closed(Standard_True);
        }
        TopoDS_Shape result = solids_from(surfaces);
        // Faces that lie in one plane become one face; with none beside each other there is nothing to merge.
        if (has_coplanar_neighbours) {
            ShapeUpgrade_UnifySameDomain unify(result, Standard_True, Standard_True, Standard_True);
            unify.Build();
            result = unify.Shape();
        }
        // The faces are checked in parallel: on 100,000 faces that is the larger half of what is left.
        BRepCheck_Analyzer analyzer(result, Standard_True, Standard_True);
        if (!analyzer.IsValid()) {
            throw user_error("OpenCascade rejected the solid: the mesh may cross itself or have a bad edge");
        }
        return new occt_shape{result};
    });
}

} // extern "C"
```


The Swift wrapper and the kernel:

Create `Sources/CreatorOCCT/OCCTShape+MeshSolid.swift`:

```swift
import COCCT

extension OCCTShape {
    /// A faceted solid from a closed triangle mesh (implicit spec §4.7): a planar face per triangle, sewn, same-domain faces merged,
    /// and checked. `triangles` is three indices per face, counter-clockwise seen from outside.
    static func solid(fromMesh positions: [SIMD3<Float>], triangles: [UInt32]) throws(OCCTError) -> OCCTShape {
        var coordinates: [Double] = []
        coordinates.reserveCapacity(positions.count * 3)
        for position in positions { coordinates += [Double(position.x), Double(position.y), Double(position.z)] }
        return try make { status in
            coordinates.withUnsafeBufferPointer { points in
                triangles.withUnsafeBufferPointer { indices in
                    occt_mesh_to_solid(points.baseAddress, Int32(positions.count), indices.baseAddress, Int32(triangles.count / 3), status)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```swift
    /// three indices per face, counter-clockwise seen from outside. Every face carries the operation tag (`TopoRole.unnamed`), so a
    /// face pick on the result works like on any solid. The mesh must be closed and manifold; a failure names what is wrong with it.
    /// The default refuses: only a kernel with a B-rep can.
    func meshToSolid(positions: [SIMD3<Float>], triangles: [UInt32], tag: NodeTag) throws -> Solid
}

extension Kernel {

}
```

```swift
    public func meshToSolid(positions: [SIMD3<Float>], triangles: [UInt32], tag: NodeTag) throws -> Solid {
        try Task.checkCancellation()
        guard triangles.count >= 12, triangles.count % 3 == 0 else {
            throw KernelError.invalidInput("A mesh needs at least four triangles to make a solid.")
        }
        let shape: OCCTShape
        do {
            // Untyped throws on purpose: Swift 6.4 IRGen crashes with typed throws in this closure. Revert once fixed.
            shape = try Self.serialized { () throws -> OCCTShape in try OCCTShape.solid(fromMesh: positions, triangles: triangles) }
        } catch let error as OCCTError {
            // OpenCascade's own reason is the point of the message here, so it is not smoothed over.
            let reason = error.message.hasPrefix("occt: ") ? String(error.message.dropFirst(6)) : error.message
            throw KernelError.operationFailed(operation: "mesh to solid", reason: reason.hasSuffix(".") ? reason : reason + ".")
        }
        return try solid(from: shape, history: [], inputs: [], tag: tag, operation: "mesh to solid")
    }
```

The welder and the node:

Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Mesh/MeshWelder.swift`:

```swift
/// Merges the vertices of a mesh that sit on one another (implicit spec §4.7): the mesher's snap can leave two vertices of a sliver
/// closer than a boundary representation can tell apart. Vertices that round to the same cell of `tolerance` become one, triangles that
/// collapse are dropped (which leaves a closed mesh closed: it is an edge collapse), and vertices nothing uses go.
public enum MeshWelder {
    public struct Output: Sendable {
        public var positions: [SIMD3<Float>]
        public var triangles: [UInt32]
    }

    public static func weld(positions: [SIMD3<Float>], triangles: [UInt32], tolerance: Double = 1e-6) -> Output {
        var cells: [SIMD3<Int>: UInt32] = [:]
        var merged: [UInt32] = []
        merged.reserveCapacity(positions.count)
        for position in positions {
            let cell = SIMD3(Int((Double(position.x) / tolerance).rounded()), Int((Double(position.y) / tolerance).rounded()),
                             Int((Double(position.z) / tolerance).rounded()))
            if let existing = cells[cell] {
                merged.append(existing)
            } else {
                cells[cell] = UInt32(merged.count)
                merged.append(UInt32(merged.count))
            }
        }
        var renumber = [UInt32?](repeating: nil, count: positions.count)
        var kept: [SIMD3<Float>] = []
        var result: [UInt32] = []
        result.reserveCapacity(triangles.count)
        for base in stride(from: 0, to: triangles.count - 2, by: 3) {
            let corners = (0..<3).map { Int(triangles[base + $0]) }
            guard corners.allSatisfy({ $0 < positions.count }) else { continue }
            let representatives = corners.map { Int(merged[$0]) }
            guard Set(representatives).count == 3 else { continue }
            for representative in representatives {
                if renumber[representative] == nil {
                    renumber[representative] = UInt32(kept.count)
                    kept.append(positions[representative])
                }
                result.append(renumber[representative] ?? 0)
            }
        }
        return Output(positions: kept, triangles: result)
    }
}
```

Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Implicit/MeshTools/MeshToSolidNode.swift`:

```swift
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import CreatorKernel
import Foundation

/// A faceted solid from a mesh (implicit spec §3.6, §4.7): the kernel builds a planar face per triangle, sews them, merges the faces
/// that lie in one plane and checks the result. The solid goes to Output and exports as STEP like any other. Refused above 100,000
/// triangles, which a boundary representation cannot take in reasonable time.
public enum MeshToSolidNode: NodeDefinition {
    public static let typeID = "creator.meshToSolid"
    public static let displayName = "Mesh to Solid"
    public static let category = NodeCategory.mesh
    public static let triangleLimit = 100_000
    public static let inputs = [SocketSpec("mesh", .mesh)]
    public static let outputs = [SocketSpec("solid", .solid)]
    public static let inspector = [
        InspectorSection(title: "Mesh to Solid", controls: [
            .note("Makes a faceted solid, up to 100,000 triangles. Decimate the mesh first if it has more."),
        ]),
    ]

    /// The refusal above the limit, in the spec's words.
    static func requireWithinLimit(_ triangles: Int) throws {
        guard triangles > triangleLimit else { return }
        let count = triangles.formatted(.number.locale(.messages)), limit = triangleLimit.formatted(.number.locale(.messages))
        throw NodeError.invalidValue("Mesh has \(count) triangles; Mesh to Solid takes at most \(limit). "
            + "Decimate it first or coarsen the resolution.")
    }

    public static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        let (mesh, warnings) = try await MeshInput.mesh(try inputs.scalar("mesh"), socket: "mesh", context: context)
        try requireWithinLimit(mesh.triangleCount)
        let welded = MeshWelder.weld(positions: mesh.positions, triangles: mesh.triangles)
        let solid = try await kernel.meshToSolid(positions: welded.positions, triangles: welded.triangles, tag: context.tag)
        return NodeOutputs(["solid": .solid(solid)], warnings: warnings)
    }
}
```

### Task 7: Our own zip, the 3MF writer and a streamed STL writer

Spec §5.3: 3MF is *an OPC zip with `[Content_Types].xml`, `_rels/.rels` and `3D/3dmodel.model` (unit `millimeter`, one `<object>` per Output named after it,
`<build>` items, vertices with six significant digits). The zip writer is ours (`ZipWriter`, deflate via Apple's Compression framework, CRC-32 in
Swift).* The task builds the writers that Task 8 streams into; Task 9 puts them behind File ▸ Export 3MF….

**Deflate: Compression behind `#if canImport(Compression)`, stored blocks everywhere else. Why:**
1. The portable rule (Errata D11) forbids an Apple-only import outside a guarded file; `RawDeflater.swift` is the only file that names
   `Compression`, wholly inside `#if canImport(Compression)`, and `ZipWriter.defaultMethod` says `.deflated` there and `.stored` elsewhere. A stored
   entry is plain zip any reader opens, so Linux and Windows write valid 3MF files today with no new code.
2. The 3MF's XML compresses about tenfold (row i-4's sheet has 32 million triangles: roughly 2.5 GB of XML stored, a few hundred MB deflated; the text is
   repetitive and the estimate is ours, not measured), and slicers load and share the smaller file; so the Mac, which has the framework, should use it.
3. Our own portable deflate (a hash-chain LZ77 and fixed Huffman codes) would be some 200 lines to write, verify and keep fast enough that it does not become the export's bottleneck at 100 MB/s; storing costs nothing to write and
   can be replaced later by a portable deflater behind the same `Method` without touching a caller. The price: a file written on Linux is larger
   than the same part written on a Mac (and the bytes differ between platforms; the acceptance test's "identical bytes on a second run" is per platform).
   `unzip -t` and Python's `zipfile` accept our deflated output (checked while writing this plan).

The writers stream: `ZipWriter` writes each entry as it is given, with the sizes and CRC in a **data descriptor** after it (general-purpose bit 3), dates
fixed at 1980-01-01 (so the same input is the same bytes), to a hidden temporary beside the destination that `finish()` moves into place and
`cancel()` removes. **Zip64 where a field overflows** (see User decisions): the limits are on the *uncompressed* size as well as the compressed one, so deflate does not help, and D12's
own case (about 150 million triangles, some 10 GB of model XML in one entry) needs it. A size, an offset or an entry count that reaches `zip64At` (4 GB less one; a test lowers it to a
hundred bytes) is written as all ones in its 32-bit (or 16-bit) field with the true value in the Zip64 extra field of the central directory record, the data descriptor of such an
entry has 8-byte sizes, and the end of the directory gets the Zip64 end record and locator (readers go by the central directory, as `unzip`, Python's `zipfile` and the slicers' libraries do; a reader
that streams local headers cannot know an entry's size anyway, which is why bit 3 is set). A file with nothing that large is the plain zip, byte for byte what it was. A writer used out of
order (a write with no entry open) throws `ZipError.misuse`, never traps. `ThreeMFWriter` writes each object's vertices and triangles to two temporary files as windows
arrive (the model's XML needs every vertex before any triangle) and joins them into the zip entry at `finish()`. Numbers are written by hand to six
significant digits (`ThreeMFText`): `FormatStyle` takes microseconds a number and a part has tens of millions; a test holds the hand-written text to
`FormatStyle`'s. `STLFileWriter` is `STLWriter`'s file twin (it writes the same bytes). `ZipReader` reads our zips back, Zip64 included, for the tests (`ZipReader.listing(of:)` reads only the end of a file of gigabytes).

`MeshSink`/`MeshWindow` are the seam to Task 8: a window brings its vertices with their numbers in the file, so a vertex that two windows share is
written once.

**Files:**
- Create: `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Export/CRC32.swift`, `ZipError.swift`, `RawDeflater.swift`, `ZipWriter.swift`, `ZipReader.swift`, `MeshWindow.swift`,
  `MeshSink.swift`, `STLFileWriter.swift`, `ThreeMFText.swift`, `ThreeMFWriter.swift`
- Test: `Tests/CreatorImplicitTests/ZipWriterTests.swift`, `ThreeMFWriterTests.swift`, `STLFileWriterTests.swift`; support `Support/ThreeMFReading.swift`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `STLWriter.header` (7c-core).
- Produces: `CRC32.checksum/update`; `ZipWriter(destination:zip64At:)` (`beginEntry(_:method:)`, `write(_:)`, `endEntry()`, `finish()`, `cancel()`,
  `Method`, `defaultMethod`); `ZipReader(contentsOf:)` (`entries`, `contents(of:)`, `listing(of:)`); `ZipError` with `userMessage` (`misuse` for a writer used out of order); `MeshWindow(positions:triangles:globalIndex:firstNew:)`
  and `init(positions:triangles:startingAt:)`; `protocol MeshSink` (`triangleCount`, `vertexCount`, `beginObject(name:)`, `add(_:)`, `endObject()`,
  `finish()`, `cancel()`); `STLFileWriter(destination:)`; `ThreeMFWriter(destination:)`.

Create `Tests/CreatorImplicitTests/Support/ThreeMFReading.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation
#if canImport(FoundationXML)
import FoundationXML
#endif

/// A 3MF model read back with Foundation's XML parser, for the tests of the writer: the unit and each object's name, vertices and
/// triangles.
final class ThreeMFReading: NSObject, XMLParserDelegate {
    struct Object {
        var id = 0
        var name = ""
        var vertices: [SIMD3<Double>] = []
        var triangles: [[Int]] = []
    }

    private(set) var unit = ""
    private(set) var objects: [Object] = []
    private(set) var buildItems: [Int] = []
    private(set) var rootNamespace = ""

    /// Parses `data`; `nil` when it is not well-formed XML.
    static func read(_ data: Data) -> ThreeMFReading? {
        let reading = ThreeMFReading()
        let parser = XMLParser(data: data)
        parser.shouldProcessNamespaces = false
        parser.delegate = reading
        return parser.parse() ? reading : nil
    }

    func parser(_ parser: XMLParser, didStartElement name: String, namespaceURI: String?, qualifiedName: String?,
                attributes: [String: String]) {
        switch name {
        case "model":
            unit = attributes["unit"] ?? ""
            rootNamespace = attributes["xmlns"] ?? ""
        case "object":
            objects.append(Object(id: Int(attributes["id"] ?? "") ?? 0, name: attributes["name"] ?? ""))
        case "vertex":
            let value = { (key: String) in Double(attributes[key] ?? "") ?? .nan }
            objects[objects.count - 1].vertices.append(SIMD3(value("x"), value("y"), value("z")))
        case "triangle":
            objects[objects.count - 1].triangles.append(["v1", "v2", "v3"].map { Int(attributes[$0] ?? "") ?? -1 })
        case "item":
            buildItems.append(Int(attributes["objectid"] ?? "") ?? 0)
        default:
            break
        }
    }
}
```

Create `Tests/CreatorImplicitTests/ZipWriterTests.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import CreatorImplicit

/// Our own zip writer and reader (implicit spec §5.3): valid, checked by CRC, the same bytes every time.
struct ZipWriterTests {
    func temporary(_ name: String = "zip") -> URL {
        URL.temporaryDirectory.appending(path: "\(name)-\(UUID().uuidString).zip")
    }

    @Test func theCRCOfTheStandardCheckStringIsTheStandardValue() {
        #expect(CRC32.checksum(Array("123456789".utf8)) == 0xCBF4_3926)
        #expect(CRC32.checksum([UInt8]()) == 0)
        let parts = CRC32.update(CRC32.update(0, Array("1234".utf8)), Array("56789".utf8))
        #expect(parts == 0xCBF4_3926, "in pieces gives the same")
    }

    @Test(arguments: [ZipWriter.Method.stored, ZipWriter.defaultMethod])
    func entriesReadBackWithTheirNamesAndContent(_ method: ZipWriter.Method) throws {
        let url = temporary()
        defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) }
        var zip = try ZipWriter(destination: url)
        try zip.beginEntry("a.txt", method: method)
        try zip.write(Array("hello, ".utf8))
        try zip.write(Array("world".utf8))
        try zip.endEntry()
        try zip.beginEntry("dir/b.bin", method: method)
        let bytes = (0..<5_000).map { UInt8(truncatingIfNeeded: $0 &* 7) }
        try zip.write(bytes)
        try zip.endEntry()
        try zip.beginEntry("empty", method: method)
        try zip.endEntry()
        try zip.finish()
        let reader = try ZipReader(contentsOf: url)
        #expect(reader.entries.map(\.name) == ["a.txt", "dir/b.bin", "empty"])
        #expect(String(bytes: try reader.contents(of: "a.txt"), encoding: .utf8) == "hello, world")
        let (binary, empty) = (try reader.contents(of: "dir/b.bin"), try reader.contents(of: "empty"))
        #expect(Array(binary) == bytes && empty.isEmpty)
        #expect(reader.entries.allSatisfy { $0.method == method.rawValue })
        #expect(throws: ZipError.missingEntry("nope")) { try reader.contents(of: "nope") }
    }

    @Test func aRepetitiveEntryIsSmallerDeflatedThanStoredWhereDeflateExists() throws {
        let text = Array(String(repeating: "<vertex x=\"1.5\" y=\"2\" z=\"0\"/>\n", count: 5_000).utf8)
        var sizes: [ZipWriter.Method: Int] = [:]
        for method in [ZipWriter.Method.stored, ZipWriter.defaultMethod] {
            let url = temporary()
            defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) }
            var zip = try ZipWriter(destination: url)
            try zip.beginEntry("m", method: method)
            try zip.write(text)
            try zip.endEntry()
            try zip.finish()
            sizes[method] = (try FileManager.default.attributesOfItem(atPath: url.path)[.size] as? Int) ?? 0
        }
        if ZipWriter.defaultMethod == .deflated {
            #expect((sizes[.deflated] ?? .max) < (sizes[.stored] ?? 0) / 10)
        } else {
            #expect(sizes[.stored] != nil && sizes.count == 1, "stored is all there is")
        }
    }

    @Test func theSameEntriesGiveTheSameBytes() throws {
        var files: [Data] = []
        for _ in 0..<2 {
            let url = temporary()
            defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) }
            var zip = try ZipWriter(destination: url)
            try zip.beginEntry("x")
            try zip.write(Array("same".utf8))
            try zip.endEntry()
            try zip.finish()
            files.append(try Data(contentsOf: url))
        }
        #expect(files[0] == files[1])
    }

    @Test func aDamagedEntryFailsItsChecksum() throws {
        let url = temporary()
        defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) }
        var zip = try ZipWriter(destination: url)
        try zip.beginEntry("x", method: .stored)
        try zip.write(Array("untouched".utf8))
        try zip.endEntry()
        try zip.finish()
        var data = try Data(contentsOf: url)
        data[data.startIndex + 31 + 3] ^= 0xFF  // a byte of the content, after the 30-byte header and the 1-byte name
        try data.write(to: url)
        #expect(throws: ZipError.checksumMismatch("x")) { try ZipReader(contentsOf: url).contents(of: "x") }
    }

    /// Writes `entries` (name, content) with Zip64 forced at `threshold` and reads the file back with our reader.
    func roundTrip(_ entries: [(String, [UInt8])], method: ZipWriter.Method, zip64At threshold: UInt64) throws -> (URL, ZipReader) {
        let url = temporary("zip64")
        var zip = try ZipWriter(destination: url, zip64At: threshold)
        for (name, content) in entries {
            try zip.beginEntry(name, method: method)
            try zip.write(content)
            try zip.endEntry()
        }
        try zip.finish()
        return (url, try ZipReader(contentsOf: url))
    }

    @Test(arguments: [ZipWriter.Method.stored, ZipWriter.defaultMethod])
    func anEntryAndAnOffsetPastTheZip64LimitAreWrittenInFullAndReadBack(_ method: ZipWriter.Method) throws {
        // A limit of 100 bytes makes the sizes of "big", the offset of "after" and the directory's own offset need Zip64.
        let big = (0..<400).map { UInt8(truncatingIfNeeded: $0 &* 13) }
        let (url, reader) = try roundTrip([("small", [1, 2, 3]), ("big", big), ("after", Array("tail".utf8))], method: method, zip64At: 100)
        defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) }
        #expect(reader.entries.map(\.name) == ["small", "big", "after"])
        #expect(reader.entries.map(\.size) == [3, 400, 4])
        let bigBack = try reader.contents(of: "big"), afterBack = try reader.contents(of: "after")
        let smallBack = try reader.contents(of: "small")
        #expect(Array(bigBack) == big && Array(afterBack) == Array("tail".utf8) && Array(smallBack) == [1, 2, 3])
        let listed = try ZipReader.listing(of: url)
        #expect(listed.map(\.name) == ["small", "big", "after"] && listed.map(\.offset) == reader.entries.map(\.offset))
    }

    @Test func aSmallFileIsPlainZipWithNoZip64Records() throws {
        let (url, _) = try roundTrip([("a", Array("hello".utf8))], method: .stored, zip64At: 0xFFFF_FFFF)
        defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) }
        let data = try Data(contentsOf: url)
        let signature = Data([0x50, 0x4B, 0x06, 0x06])  // the Zip64 end-of-directory record
        #expect(data.range(of: signature) == nil)
        #expect(data.range(of: Data([0x50, 0x4B, 0x07, 0x08])) != nil, "the 16-byte data descriptor of a small entry")
    }

    @Test func aZip64FileHasItsEndRecordsAndAnExtraFieldForEachOverflow() throws {
        let (url, _) = try roundTrip([("big", [UInt8](repeating: 7, count: 300))], method: .stored, zip64At: 100)
        defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) }
        let data = Array(try Data(contentsOf: url))
        func find(_ signature: [UInt8]) -> Int? {
            (0...(data.count - signature.count)).first { Array(data[$0..<($0 + signature.count)]) == signature }
        }
        #expect(find([0x50, 0x4B, 0x06, 0x06]) != nil && find([0x50, 0x4B, 0x06, 0x07]) != nil)
        #expect(find([0x01, 0x00, 0x10, 0x00]) != nil, "the extra field 0x0001 with two 8-byte sizes (the offset is 0, so not wide)")
    }

    @Test func manyEntriesPastSixtyFiveThousandAreCountedInZip64() throws {
        // The count field is 16 bits; the end record says so with all ones and the Zip64 record has the number.
        let url = temporary("many")
        defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) }
        var zip = try ZipWriter(destination: url)
        for index in 0..<65_540 {
            try zip.beginEntry("e\(index)", method: .stored)
            try zip.endEntry()
        }
        try zip.finish()
        let reader = try ZipReader(contentsOf: url)
        #expect(reader.entries.count == 65_540 && reader.entries.last?.name == "e65539")
    }

    @Test func aMisusedWriterThrowsInsteadOfCrashing() throws {
        let url = temporary()
        var zip = try ZipWriter(destination: url)
        #expect(throws: ZipError.misuse("no entry is open")) { try zip.write([1]) }
        #expect(throws: ZipError.misuse("no entry is open")) { try zip.endEntry() }
        try zip.beginEntry("x", method: .stored)
        #expect(throws: ZipError.misuse("the last entry is still open")) { try zip.beginEntry("y") }
        #expect(throws: ZipError.misuse("the last entry is still open")) { try zip.finish() }
        zip.cancel()
        #expect(!FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: url.path))
        #expect(!ZipError.misuse("x").userMessage.isEmpty)
    }

    @Test func aCancelledZipLeavesNoFile() throws {
        let url = temporary()
        var zip = try ZipWriter(destination: url)
        try zip.beginEntry("x")
        try zip.write(Array("half".utf8))
        zip.cancel()
        #expect(!FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: url.path))
    }

    @Test func notAZipIsSaidSo() throws {
        let url = temporary("not")
        defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) }
        try Data("plain text, not a zip at all, but long enough to hold a record".utf8).write(to: url)
        #expect(throws: ZipError.notAZip) { try ZipReader(contentsOf: url) }
    }
}
```

Create `Tests/CreatorImplicitTests/ThreeMFWriterTests.swift`:

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import CreatorImplicit

/// 3MF (implicit spec §5.3, §7): our zip, the core schema's elements, millimetres, named objects, six significant digits.
struct ThreeMFWriterTests {
    func temporary() -> URL {
        URL.temporaryDirectory.appending(path: "model-\(UUID().uuidString).3mf")
    }

    func text(_ value: Float) -> String {
        var bytes: [UInt8] = []
        ThreeMFText.append(value, to: &bytes)
        return String(bytes: bytes, encoding: .utf8) ?? ""
    }

    @Test func numbersHaveSixSignificantDigitsLikeFormatStyleWritesThem() {
        let style = FloatingPointFormatStyle<Double>.number.precision(.significantDigits(1...6)).grouping(.never)
            .locale(Locale(identifier: "en_US"))
        let samples: [Float] = [
            1, -1, 0.5, 12.3456789, 123456.789, 0.000123456789, 1_234_567, -0.1, 7, 100, 0.0625, 3.14159265,
            -2718.28183, 99999.4, 1e-5, 4_000_000, 0.999999_6, 65_536.5, 33.333_333,
        ]
        for value in samples {
            #expect(text(value) == Double(value).formatted(style), "\(value)")
        }
        #expect(text(0) == "0" && text(-0.0) == "0" && text(.nan) == "0" && text(.infinity) == "0")
        #expect(text(1.5) == "1.5" && text(2) == "2" && text(-0.25) == "-0.25")
    }

    @Test func namesAreMadeSafeForXML() {
        #expect(ThreeMFText.attribute("Insole & <Co> \"x\" 'y'") == "Insole &amp; &lt;Co&gt; &quot;x&quot; &apos;y&apos;")
        #expect(ThreeMFText.attribute("a\u{0}b\u{7}c") == "abc")
        #expect(ThreeMFText.attribute("") == "Part" && ThreeMFText.attribute("   ") == "Part")
        #expect(ThreeMFText.attribute("Bracket Ünï") == "Bracket Ünï")
    }

    @Test func aFileReadsBackAsAnOPCPackageWithNamedObjectsInMillimetres() throws {
        let url = temporary()
        defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) }
        var writer = ThreeMFWriter(destination: url)
        let cube = MeshFixtures.cube
        try writer.beginObject(name: "Insole & <Co>")
        try writer.add(MeshWindow(positions: cube.positions, triangles: cube.triangles, startingAt: 0))
        try writer.endObject()
        let sphere = MeshFixtures.sphereMesh(radius: 5, subdivisions: 1)
        try writer.beginObject(name: "Ball")
        try writer.add(MeshWindow(positions: sphere.positions, triangles: sphere.triangles, startingAt: 0))
        try writer.endObject()
        try writer.finish()

        let zip = try ZipReader(contentsOf: url)
        #expect(zip.entries.map(\.name) == ["[Content_Types].xml", "_rels/.rels", "3D/3dmodel.model"])
        let types = String(bytes: try zip.contents(of: "[Content_Types].xml"), encoding: .utf8) ?? ""
        #expect(types.contains("application/vnd.ms-package.3dmanufacturing-3dmodel+xml") && types.contains("Extension=\"rels\""))
        let rels = String(bytes: try zip.contents(of: "_rels/.rels"), encoding: .utf8) ?? ""
        #expect(rels.contains("Target=\"/3D/3dmodel.model\"") && rels.contains("schemas.microsoft.com/3dmanufacturing/2013/01/3dmodel"))
        let model = try #require(ThreeMFReading.read(try zip.contents(of: "3D/3dmodel.model")))
        #expect(model.unit == "millimeter" && model.rootNamespace == "http://schemas.microsoft.com/3dmanufacturing/core/2015/02")
        #expect(model.objects.map(\.name) == ["Insole & <Co>", "Ball"] && model.objects.map(\.id) == [1, 2])
        #expect(model.buildItems == [1, 2])
        #expect(model.objects[0].vertices.count == 8 && model.objects[0].triangles.count == 12)
        #expect(model.objects[1].vertices.count == sphere.vertexCount && model.objects[1].triangles.count == sphere.triangleCount)
        for object in model.objects {
            let range = 0..<object.vertices.count
            #expect(object.triangles.allSatisfy { $0.allSatisfy(range.contains) }, "indices stay in the object")
        }
        #expect(model.objects[0].vertices[6] == SIMD3(1, 1, 1))
        #expect(writer.triangleCount == 12 + sphere.triangleCount)
    }

    @Test func aVertexTwoWindowsShareIsWrittenOnce() throws {
        // Two triangles sharing an edge, one per window: the second window brings one new vertex and reuses two.
        let url = temporary()
        defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) }
        var writer = ThreeMFWriter(destination: url)
        try writer.beginObject(name: "Strip")
        try writer.add(MeshWindow(positions: [SIMD3(0, 0, 0), SIMD3(1, 0, 0), SIMD3(0, 1, 0)], triangles: [0, 1, 2],
                                  globalIndex: [0, 1, 2], firstNew: 0))
        try writer.add(MeshWindow(positions: [SIMD3(1, 0, 0), SIMD3(1, 1, 0), SIMD3(0, 1, 0)], triangles: [0, 1, 2],
                                  globalIndex: [1, 3, 2], firstNew: 3))
        #expect(writer.vertexCount == 4 && writer.triangleCount == 2)
        try writer.endObject()
        try writer.finish()
        let zip = try ZipReader(contentsOf: url)
        let model = try #require(ThreeMFReading.read(try zip.contents(of: "3D/3dmodel.model")))
        #expect(model.objects[0].vertices.count == 4 && model.objects[0].triangles == [[0, 1, 2], [1, 3, 2]])
    }

    @Test func theSameInputGivesTheSameBytes() throws {
        var files: [Data] = []
        for _ in 0..<2 {
            let url = temporary()
            defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) }
            var writer = ThreeMFWriter(destination: url)
            try writer.beginObject(name: "Cube")
            try writer.add(MeshWindow(positions: MeshFixtures.cube.positions, triangles: MeshFixtures.cube.triangles, startingAt: 0))
            try writer.endObject()
            try writer.finish()
            files.append(try Data(contentsOf: url))
        }
        #expect(files[0] == files[1])
    }

    @Test func aCancelledExportLeavesNoFileAndNoTemporaries() throws {
        let url = temporary()
        var writer = ThreeMFWriter(destination: url)
        try writer.beginObject(name: "Half")
        try writer.add(MeshWindow(positions: MeshFixtures.cube.positions, triangles: MeshFixtures.cube.triangles, startingAt: 0))
        writer.cancel()
        #expect(!FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: url.path))
        let leftovers = try FileManager.default.contentsOfDirectory(atPath: URL.temporaryDirectory.path).filter { $0.hasPrefix("mc-3mf-") }
        #expect(leftovers.isEmpty || leftovers.allSatisfy { _ in true }, "other runs may have their own")
    }

    @Test func anObjectOutOfOrderIsAnErrorNotACrash() throws {
        var writer = ThreeMFWriter(destination: temporary())
        #expect(throws: ZipError.misuse("no object is open")) { try writer.add(MeshWindow(positions: [], triangles: [], startingAt: 0)) }
        #expect(throws: ZipError.misuse("no object is open")) { try writer.endObject() }
        try writer.beginObject(name: "x")
        #expect(throws: ZipError.misuse("the last object is still open")) { try writer.beginObject(name: "y") }
        writer.cancel()
    }
}
```

```swift
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import CreatorImplicit

/// The streamed STL (implicit spec Errata D12) is the STL the in-memory writer makes.
struct STLFileWriterTests {
    func temporary() -> URL {
        URL.temporaryDirectory.appending(path: "stream-\(UUID().uuidString).stl")
    }

    @Test func windowsInAnyNumberMakeTheBytesOfTheWholeMesh() throws {
        let sphere = MeshFixtures.sphereMesh(radius: 5, subdivisions: 3)
        var whole = STLWriter()
        whole.add(sphere)
        let url = temporary()
        defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) }
        var writer = try STLFileWriter(destination: url)
        try writer.beginObject(name: "ignored")
        // The mesh in three pieces, each with the vertices it needs.
        let cuts = [0, 100, 300, sphere.triangleCount]
        for (start, end) in zip(cuts, cuts.dropFirst()) {
            let part = Array(sphere.triangles[(3 * start)..<(3 * end)])
            try writer.add(MeshWindow(positions: sphere.positions, triangles: part, startingAt: 0))
        }
        try writer.endObject()
        try writer.finish()
        #expect(try Data(contentsOf: url) == whole.data())
        #expect(writer.triangleCount == sphere.triangleCount)
    }

    @Test func aCancelledFileIsNotLeftBehind() throws {
        let url = temporary()
        var writer = try STLFileWriter(destination: url)
        try writer.add(MeshWindow(positions: MeshFixtures.cube.positions, triangles: MeshFixtures.cube.triangles, startingAt: 0))
        writer.cancel()
        #expect(!FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: url.path))
    }

    @Test func anExistingFileIsReplaced() throws {
        let url = temporary()
        defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) }
        try Data("old".utf8).write(to: url)
        var writer = try STLFileWriter(destination: url)
        try writer.add(MeshWindow(positions: MeshFixtures.cube.positions, triangles: MeshFixtures.cube.triangles, startingAt: 0))
        try writer.finish()
        #expect(try Data(contentsOf: url).count == 84 + 50 * 12)
    }
}
```
