Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Export/CRC32.swift`:

```swift
/// CRC-32 (IEEE 802.3, the polynomial every zip reader checks), by table, in pieces.
public enum CRC32 {
    private static let table: [UInt32] = (0..<256).map { index -> UInt32 in
        var value = UInt32(index)
        for _ in 0..<8 { value = value & 1 == 1 ? 0xEDB8_8320 ^ (value >> 1) : value >> 1 }
        return value
    }

    /// The running value for `bytes` after `crc` (start from 0).
    public static func update(_ crc: UInt32, _ bytes: some Sequence<UInt8>) -> UInt32 {
        var value = ~crc
        for byte in bytes { value = table[Int((value ^ UInt32(byte)) & 0xFF)] ^ (value >> 8) }
        return ~value
    }

    /// The CRC-32 of `bytes`.
    public static func checksum(_ bytes: some Sequence<UInt8>) -> UInt32 {
        update(0, bytes)
    }
}
```

Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Export/ZipError.swift`:

```swift
/// Why a zip could not be written or read.
public enum ZipError: Error, Equatable, Sendable {
    /// The writer was used out of order (an entry written to before it was opened): a bug in the caller, reported rather than trapped.
    case misuse(String)
    case compressionFailed
    case notAZip
    case missingEntry(String)
    case checksumMismatch(String)
    case unsupportedMethod(String)
    case writeFailed(String)

    public var userMessage: String {
        switch self {
        case .misuse(let what):
            "The file couldn't be written: \(what)."
        case .compressionFailed:
            "The 3MF file couldn't be compressed."
        case .notAZip:
            "The file is not a zip."
        case .missingEntry(let name):
            "The zip has no “\(name)”."
        case .checksumMismatch(let name):
            "“\(name)” in the zip is damaged."
        case .unsupportedMethod(let name):
            "“\(name)” in the zip is compressed in a way this build can't read."
        case .writeFailed(let reason):
            "The file couldn't be written: \(reason)"
        }
    }
}
```

Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Export/RawDeflater.swift`:

```swift
#if canImport(Compression)
import Compression

/// Raw DEFLATE (RFC 1951), the compression a zip entry of method 8 holds, by Apple's Compression framework: `COMPRESSION_ZLIB` is
/// deflate without the zlib header. The only file that names the framework; elsewhere zip entries are stored (`ZipWriter.defaultMethod`).
final class RawDeflater {
    private static let bufferSize = 1 << 16
    private var stream: compression_stream
    private let output: UnsafeMutablePointer<UInt8>
    private let placeholder: UnsafeMutablePointer<UInt8>

    init() throws {
        output = UnsafeMutablePointer<UInt8>.allocate(capacity: Self.bufferSize)
        placeholder = UnsafeMutablePointer<UInt8>.allocate(capacity: 1)
        stream = compression_stream(dst_ptr: output, dst_size: Self.bufferSize, src_ptr: UnsafePointer(placeholder), src_size: 0,
                                    state: nil)
        guard compression_stream_init(&stream, COMPRESSION_STREAM_ENCODE, COMPRESSION_ZLIB) == COMPRESSION_STATUS_OK else {
            output.deallocate()
            placeholder.deallocate()
            throw ZipError.compressionFailed
        }
    }

    deinit {
        compression_stream_destroy(&stream)
        output.deallocate()
        placeholder.deallocate()
    }

    /// Compresses `input`, handing every piece of output to `emit`. With `finish`, the stream is ended and everything left is emitted.
    func process(_ input: UnsafeRawBufferPointer, finish: Bool, emit: (UnsafeBufferPointer<UInt8>) throws -> Void) throws {
        stream.src_ptr = input.baseAddress.map { UnsafePointer($0.assumingMemoryBound(to: UInt8.self)) } ?? UnsafePointer(placeholder)
        stream.src_size = input.count
        let flags: Int32 = finish ? Int32(COMPRESSION_STREAM_FINALIZE.rawValue) : 0
        while true {
            stream.dst_ptr = output
            stream.dst_size = Self.bufferSize
            let status = compression_stream_process(&stream, flags)
            guard status != COMPRESSION_STATUS_ERROR else { throw ZipError.compressionFailed }
            let produced = Self.bufferSize - stream.dst_size
            if produced > 0 { try emit(UnsafeBufferPointer(start: output, count: produced)) }
            if status == COMPRESSION_STATUS_END || (!finish && stream.src_size == 0 && stream.dst_size > 0) { break }
        }
    }
}
#endif
```

Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Export/ZipWriter.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// Writes a zip file entry by entry, streaming (implicit spec §5.3): each entry's data goes to the file as it is given, with its
/// sizes and CRC in a data descriptor after it, so an entry of any length needs no memory. Entries are deflated where Apple's
/// Compression framework exists (`defaultMethod`) and stored everywhere else; both are plain zip that any reader opens. The file
/// is written to a temporary beside the destination and moved into place by `finish()`, so a cancelled export leaves nothing, and
/// dates are fixed, so the same entries give the same bytes. A file, an entry or an offset that reaches 4 GB is written as Zip64, so a
/// 3MF of tens of gigabytes of XML (implicit spec Errata D12's case) is a valid zip: only the fields that overflow change, and a small
/// file is the plain zip every reader knows.
public struct ZipWriter {
    public enum Method: UInt16, Sendable {
        case stored = 0
        case deflated = 8
    }

    /// Deflated where the Compression framework exists, else stored. Stored blocks need no code and run anywhere, but a 3MF's XML is
    /// about ten times larger stored.
    public static var defaultMethod: Method {
        #if canImport(Compression)
        .deflated
        #else
        .stored
        #endif
    }

    private struct Entry {
        var name: String
        var method: Method
        var crc: UInt32
        var compressedSize: UInt64
        var size: UInt64
        var offset: UInt64
    }

    private struct Current {
        var name: String
        var method: Method
        var crc: UInt32 = 0
        var compressedSize: UInt64 = 0
        var size: UInt64 = 0
        var offset: UInt64
        #if canImport(Compression)
        var deflater: RawDeflater?
        #endif
    }

    /// The value from which a size, an offset or a count no longer fits its 32-bit (or 16-bit) field and is written as Zip64. A field
    /// holding all ones means "see the Zip64 extra", so 4 GB less one is already too large. The tests lower it.
    public let zip64At: UInt64
    private let destination: URL
    private let partial: URL
    private var handle: FileHandle?
    private var offset: UInt64 = 0
    private var entries: [Entry] = []
    private var current: Current?
    private var buffer: [UInt8] = []

    /// Starts a zip that `finish()` will put at `destination`.
    public init(destination: URL, zip64At: UInt64 = 0xFFFF_FFFF) throws {
        self.zip64At = zip64At
        self.destination = destination
        partial = destination.deletingLastPathComponent().appending(path: ".\(UUID().uuidString).partial")
        guard FileManager.default.createFile(atPath: partial.path, contents: nil) else {
            throw ZipError.writeFailed("the folder can't be written to.")
        }
        handle = try FileHandle(forWritingTo: partial)
    }

    /// Opens an entry; what is `write`n until `endEntry()` is its content.
    public mutating func beginEntry(_ name: String, method: Method = ZipWriter.defaultMethod) throws {
        guard current == nil else { throw ZipError.misuse("the last entry is still open") }
        let nameBytes = Array(name.utf8)
        var header: [UInt8] = []
        header.append(contentsOf: Self.u32(0x0403_4B50))
        header.append(contentsOf: Self.u16(20))
        header.append(contentsOf: Self.u16(0x0808))  // sizes follow the data; the name is UTF-8
        header.append(contentsOf: Self.u16(method.rawValue))
        header.append(contentsOf: Self.u16(0))  // time: midnight
        header.append(contentsOf: Self.u16(0x0021))  // date: 1980-01-01
        header.append(contentsOf: Self.u32(0) + Self.u32(0) + Self.u32(0))
        header.append(contentsOf: Self.u16(UInt16(nameBytes.count)))
        header.append(contentsOf: Self.u16(0))
        header.append(contentsOf: nameBytes)
        current = Current(name: name, method: method, offset: offset)
        #if canImport(Compression)
        if method == .deflated { current?.deflater = try RawDeflater() }
        #endif
        try put(header)
    }

    /// Adds to the open entry.
    public mutating func write(_ bytes: [UInt8]) throws {
        guard var entry = current else { throw ZipError.misuse("no entry is open") }
        entry.crc = CRC32.update(entry.crc, bytes)
        entry.size += UInt64(bytes.count)
        current = entry
        switch entry.method {
        case .stored:
            current?.compressedSize += UInt64(bytes.count)
            try put(bytes)
        case .deflated:
            try deflate(bytes, finish: false)
        }
    }

    /// Ends the open entry, with its descriptor.
    public mutating func endEntry() throws {
        guard let entry = current else { throw ZipError.misuse("no entry is open") }
        if entry.method == .deflated { try deflate([], finish: true) }
        guard let done = current else { return }
        // The descriptor holds 8-byte sizes when either size needs them (Zip64); readers go by the central directory.
        if done.compressedSize >= zip64At || done.size >= zip64At {
            try put(Self.u32(0x0807_4B50) + Self.u32(done.crc) + Self.u64(done.compressedSize) + Self.u64(done.size))
        } else {
            try put(Self.u32(0x0807_4B50) + Self.u32(done.crc) + Self.u32(UInt32(done.compressedSize)) + Self.u32(UInt32(done.size)))
        }
        entries.append(Entry(name: done.name, method: done.method, crc: done.crc, compressedSize: done.compressedSize,
                             size: done.size, offset: done.offset))
        current = nil
    }

    /// Writes the central directory, closes the file and moves it to its destination.
    public mutating func finish() throws {
        guard current == nil else { throw ZipError.misuse("the last entry is still open") }
        let start = offset
        for entry in entries { try put(centralRecord(for: entry)) }
        let size = offset - start
        if entries.count >= 0xFFFF || start >= zip64At || size >= zip64At {
            let recordOffset = offset
            try put(Self.u32(0x0606_4B50) + Self.u64(44) + Self.u16(45) + Self.u16(45) + Self.u32(0) + Self.u32(0)
                + Self.u64(UInt64(entries.count)) + Self.u64(UInt64(entries.count)) + Self.u64(size) + Self.u64(start))
            try put(Self.u32(0x0706_4B50) + Self.u32(0) + Self.u64(recordOffset) + Self.u32(1))
        }
        let count = UInt16(min(entries.count, 0xFFFF))
        try put(Self.u32(0x0605_4B50) + Self.u16(0) + Self.u16(0) + Self.u16(count) + Self.u16(count)
            + Self.u32(size >= zip64At ? 0xFFFF_FFFF : UInt32(size)) + Self.u32(start >= zip64At ? 0xFFFF_FFFF : UInt32(start))
            + Self.u16(0))
        try flush()
        try handle?.close()
        handle = nil
        let manager = FileManager.default
        if manager.fileExists(atPath: destination.path) { try manager.removeItem(at: destination) }
        try manager.moveItem(at: partial, to: destination)
    }

    /// An entry's record in the central directory; the sizes and the offset that overflow say so with all ones and are given in full in
    /// the Zip64 extra field (RFC-style order: size, compressed size, offset).
    private func centralRecord(for entry: Entry) -> [UInt8] {
        let nameBytes = Array(entry.name.utf8)
        let wide = [entry.size, entry.compressedSize, entry.offset].map { $0 >= zip64At }
        var extra: [UInt8] = []
        if wide.contains(true) {
            var body: [UInt8] = []
            if wide[0] { body += Self.u64(entry.size) }
            if wide[1] { body += Self.u64(entry.compressedSize) }
            if wide[2] { body += Self.u64(entry.offset) }
            extra = Self.u16(0x0001) + Self.u16(UInt16(body.count)) + body
        }
        let field = { (value: UInt64, isWide: Bool) in Self.u32(isWide ? 0xFFFF_FFFF : UInt32(value)) }
        var record: [UInt8] = []
        record += Self.u32(0x0201_4B50) + Self.u16(extra.isEmpty ? 20 : 45) + Self.u16(extra.isEmpty ? 20 : 45) + Self.u16(0x0808)
        record += Self.u16(entry.method.rawValue) + Self.u16(0) + Self.u16(0x0021) + Self.u32(entry.crc)
        record += field(entry.compressedSize, wide[1]) + field(entry.size, wide[0])
        record += Self.u16(UInt16(nameBytes.count)) + Self.u16(UInt16(extra.count)) + Self.u16(0)
        record += Self.u16(0) + Self.u16(0) + Self.u32(0) + field(entry.offset, wide[2]) + nameBytes + extra
        return record
    }

    /// Throws away what was written.
    public mutating func cancel() {
        try? handle?.close()
        handle = nil
        try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: partial)
    }

    // MARK: Output

    private mutating func put(_ bytes: [UInt8]) throws {
        buffer += bytes
        offset += UInt64(bytes.count)
        if buffer.count >= 1 << 20 { try flush() }
    }

    private mutating func flush() throws {
        guard !buffer.isEmpty else { return }
        do {
            try handle?.write(contentsOf: buffer)
        } catch {
            throw ZipError.writeFailed(error.localizedDescription)
        }
        buffer.removeAll(keepingCapacity: true)
    }

    private mutating func deflate(_ bytes: [UInt8], finish: Bool) throws {
        #if canImport(Compression)
        guard let deflater = current?.deflater else { return }
        var pieces: [[UInt8]] = []
        try bytes.withUnsafeBytes { input in
            try deflater.process(input, finish: finish) { pieces.append(Array($0)) }
        }
        for piece in pieces {
            current?.compressedSize += UInt64(piece.count)
            try put(piece)
        }
        #else
        throw ZipError.compressionFailed
        #endif
    }

    static func u16(_ value: UInt16) -> [UInt8] { [UInt8(value & 0xFF), UInt8(value >> 8)] }

    static func u32(_ value: UInt32) -> [UInt8] { (0..<4).map { UInt8(truncatingIfNeeded: value >> (8 * UInt32($0))) } }

    static func u64(_ value: UInt64) -> [UInt8] { (0..<8).map { UInt8(truncatingIfNeeded: value >> (8 * UInt64($0))) } }
}
```

Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Export/ZipReader.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation
#if canImport(Compression)
import Compression
#endif

/// Reads a zip written by `ZipWriter` (Zip64 included): the central directory, then each entry checked against its CRC. The tests read a
/// 3MF back with it; the app does not need to read zips. It holds the whole file, so it is for small files; a Zip64 file of gigabytes is
/// listed by `ZipReader.listing(of:)`, which reads the end of the file alone.
public struct ZipReader {
    public struct Entry: Sendable {
        public var name: String
        public var method: UInt16
        public var crc: UInt32
        public var compressedSize: Int
        public var size: Int
        public var offset: Int
    }

    public let entries: [Entry]
    private let data: Data

    public init(contentsOf url: URL) throws {
        data = try Data(contentsOf: url)
        entries = try Self.directory(of: data)
    }

    /// The entries of a zip, read from its end alone: the last megabyte holds the central directory of any zip this app writes, so a file
    /// of gigabytes is listed without being read.
    public static func listing(of url: URL) throws -> [Entry] {
        let handle = try FileHandle(forReadingFrom: url)
        defer { try? handle.close() }
        let length = try handle.seekToEnd()
        let tail = min(length, 1 << 20)
        try handle.seek(toOffset: length - tail)
        let data = try handle.read(upToCount: Int(tail)) ?? Data()
        return try directory(of: data, offsetOfData: Int(length - tail))
    }

    /// Parses the end records and the central directory of `data`, a whole file or the tail of one that starts at `offsetOfData`.
    private static func directory(of data: Data, offsetOfData: Int = 0) throws -> [Entry] {
        guard data.count >= 22, let end = endOfDirectory(in: data) else { throw ZipError.notAZip }
        var (count, size, position) = (Int(u16(data, end + 10)), Int(u32(data, end + 12)), Int(u32(data, end + 16)))
        // All ones in a field of the end record say the numbers are in the Zip64 end record, found by the locator just before.
        let wide = count == 0xFFFF || position == 0xFFFF_FFFF || size == 0xFFFF_FFFF
        if wide, end >= 20, u32(data, end - 20) == 0x0706_4B50 {
            let record = Int(u64(data, end - 20 + 8)) - offsetOfData
            guard record >= 0, record + 56 <= data.count, u32(data, record) == 0x0606_4B50 else { throw ZipError.notAZip }
            count = Int(u64(data, record + 32))
            size = Int(u64(data, record + 40))
            position = Int(u64(data, record + 48))
        }
        position -= offsetOfData
        guard position >= 0, size >= 0 else { throw ZipError.notAZip }
        var found: [Entry] = []
        for _ in 0..<count {
            let (entry, length) = try record(at: position, in: data)
            found.append(entry)
            position += length
        }
        return found
    }

    /// The central directory record at `position`, and its length.
    private static func record(at position: Int, in data: Data) throws -> (Entry, Int) {
        guard position + 46 <= data.count, u32(data, position) == 0x0201_4B50 else { throw ZipError.notAZip }
        let nameLength = Int(u16(data, position + 28)), extraLength = Int(u16(data, position + 30))
        let comment = Int(u16(data, position + 32))
        guard position + 46 + nameLength + extraLength <= data.count else { throw ZipError.notAZip }
        let name = String(bytes: data[(position + 46)..<(position + 46 + nameLength)], encoding: .utf8) ?? ""
        var entry = Entry(name: name, method: u16(data, position + 10), crc: u32(data, position + 16),
                          compressedSize: Int(u32(data, position + 20)), size: Int(u32(data, position + 24)),
                          offset: Int(u32(data, position + 42)))
        // The Zip64 extra holds, in this order, the fields that were all ones: size, compressed size, offset.
        var field = position + 46 + nameLength
        let extraEnd = field + extraLength
        while field + 4 <= extraEnd {
            let (id, length) = (u16(data, field), Int(u16(data, field + 2)))
            if id == 0x0001 {
                var at = field + 4
                for keyPath in [\Entry.size, \Entry.compressedSize, \Entry.offset] where entry[keyPath: keyPath] == 0xFFFF_FFFF {
                    guard at + 8 <= extraEnd else { throw ZipError.notAZip }
                    entry[keyPath: keyPath] = Int(u64(data, at))
                    at += 8
                }
            }
            field += 4 + length
        }
        return (entry, 46 + nameLength + extraLength + comment)
    }

    /// The content of `name`, inflated and checked against its CRC.
    public func contents(of name: String) throws -> Data {
        guard let entry = entries.first(where: { $0.name == name }) else { throw ZipError.missingEntry(name) }
        let nameLength = Int(Self.u16(data, entry.offset + 26)), extra = Int(Self.u16(data, entry.offset + 28))
        let start = entry.offset + 30 + nameLength + extra
        guard start + entry.compressedSize <= data.count else { throw ZipError.notAZip }
        let stored = data[start..<(start + entry.compressedSize)]
        let content: Data
        switch entry.method {
        case 0:
            content = Data(stored)
        case 8:
            content = try Self.inflate(stored, size: entry.size, name: name)
        default:
            throw ZipError.unsupportedMethod(name)
        }
        guard content.count == entry.size, CRC32.checksum(content) == entry.crc else { throw ZipError.checksumMismatch(name) }
        return content
    }

    private static func inflate(_ compressed: Data.SubSequence, size: Int, name: String) throws -> Data {
        #if canImport(Compression)
        guard size > 0 else { return Data() }
        var output = Data(count: size)
        let written = output.withUnsafeMutableBytes { destination -> Int in
            compressed.withUnsafeBytes { source -> Int in
                guard let to = destination.bindMemory(to: UInt8.self).baseAddress,
                      let from = source.bindMemory(to: UInt8.self).baseAddress else { return 0 }
                return compression_decode_buffer(to, size, from, source.count, nil, COMPRESSION_ZLIB)
            }
        }
        guard written == size else { throw ZipError.checksumMismatch(name) }
        return output
        #else
        throw ZipError.unsupportedMethod(name)
        #endif
    }

    private static func endOfDirectory(in data: Data) -> Int? {
        var position = data.count - 22
        let lowest = max(0, data.count - 22 - 65_535)
        while position >= lowest {
            if u32(data, position) == 0x0605_4B50 { return position }
            position -= 1
        }
        return nil
    }

    private static func u16(_ data: Data, _ at: Int) -> UInt16 {
        UInt16(data[data.startIndex + at]) | UInt16(data[data.startIndex + at + 1]) << 8
    }

    private static func u32(_ data: Data, _ at: Int) -> UInt32 {
        UInt32(u16(data, at)) | UInt32(u16(data, at + 2)) << 16
    }

    private static func u64(_ data: Data, _ at: Int) -> UInt64 {
        UInt64(u32(data, at)) | UInt64(u32(data, at + 4)) << 32
    }
}
```

Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Export/MeshWindow.swift`:

```swift
/// A piece of a mesh on its way to a file (implicit spec Errata D12): the vertices and triangles of a run of bricks, with each vertex's
/// number in the whole mesh, so a vertex that two windows share is written once. `positions` and `triangles` are the window's own
/// (the triangles index `positions`); `globalIndex[i]` is the number of vertex `i` in the file, and the vertices numbered from
/// `firstNew` up are the ones the file has not seen, in order.
public struct MeshWindow: Sendable {
    public var positions: [SIMD3<Float>]
    public var triangles: [UInt32]
    public var globalIndex: [UInt32]
    public var firstNew: UInt32

    public init(positions: [SIMD3<Float>], triangles: [UInt32], globalIndex: [UInt32], firstNew: UInt32) {
        self.positions = positions
        self.triangles = triangles
        self.globalIndex = globalIndex
        self.firstNew = firstNew
    }

    /// A mesh of its own, all new: its vertices are numbered from `start`, the number of vertices the file already holds.
    public init(positions: [SIMD3<Float>], triangles: [UInt32], startingAt start: Int) {
        self.init(positions: positions, triangles: triangles, globalIndex: positions.indices.map { UInt32(start + $0) },
                  firstNew: UInt32(start))
    }

    public var triangleCount: Int { triangles.count / 3 }
}
```

Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Export/MeshSink.swift`:

```swift
/// Where a streamed export puts its triangles (implicit spec Errata D12): one window at a time, never the whole mesh. An STL sink
/// writes the triangles as they come; a 3MF sink writes the vertices and the triangles of each object to files of their own and
/// puts them in the zip at the end. `beginObject` names what the following windows are (3MF: an `<object>`; STL has one body).
public protocol MeshSink {
    /// Triangles added to the file so far.
    var triangleCount: Int { get }
    /// Vertices added to the current object so far: the number the next independent mesh's vertices start at.
    var vertexCount: Int { get }
    mutating func beginObject(name: String) throws
    mutating func add(_ window: MeshWindow) throws
    mutating func endObject() throws
    /// Completes the file and puts it at its destination.
    mutating func finish() throws
    /// Throws away what was written.
    mutating func cancel()
}
```

Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Export/STLFileWriter.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// Binary STL written to a file as triangles arrive (implicit spec Errata D12), the same bytes `STLWriter` makes for the same
/// triangles: the 80-byte header, the count (patched in when the file is finished), then 50 bytes a triangle. It writes to a temporary
/// beside the destination and moves it into place in `finish()`.
public struct STLFileWriter: MeshSink {
    private let destination: URL
    private let partial: URL
    private var handle: FileHandle?
    private var buffer: [UInt8] = []
    public private(set) var triangleCount = 0
    public let vertexCount = 0

    public init(destination: URL) throws {
        self.destination = destination
        partial = destination.deletingLastPathComponent().appending(path: ".\(UUID().uuidString).partial")
        guard FileManager.default.createFile(atPath: partial.path, contents: nil) else {
            throw ZipError.writeFailed("the folder can't be written to.")
        }
        handle = try FileHandle(forWritingTo: partial)
        buffer = STLWriter.header + [0, 0, 0, 0]
    }

    public mutating func beginObject(name: String) throws {}

    public mutating func endObject() throws {}

    public mutating func add(_ window: MeshWindow) throws {
        buffer.reserveCapacity(buffer.count + 50 * window.triangleCount)
        for base in stride(from: 0, to: window.triangles.count - 2, by: 3) {
            let (a, b, c) = (window.positions[Int(window.triangles[base])], window.positions[Int(window.triangles[base + 1])],
                             window.positions[Int(window.triangles[base + 2])])
            let (u, v) = (b - a, c - a)
            let cross = SIMD3(u.y * v.z - u.z * v.y, u.z * v.x - u.x * v.z, u.x * v.y - u.y * v.x)
            let length = (cross * cross).sum().squareRoot()
            let normal = length > 0 ? cross / length : SIMD3<Float>.zero
            for vector in [normal, a, b, c] {
                for component in [vector.x, vector.y, vector.z] {
                    buffer += ZipWriter.u32(component.bitPattern)
                }
            }
            buffer += [0, 0]
            triangleCount += 1
        }
        if buffer.count >= 1 << 20 { try flush() }
    }

    public mutating func finish() throws {
        try flush()
        guard triangleCount <= Int(UInt32.max) else { throw ZipError.writeFailed("an STL file holds at most 4,294,967,295 triangles.") }
        do {
            try handle?.seek(toOffset: 80)
            try handle?.write(contentsOf: ZipWriter.u32(UInt32(triangleCount)))
            try handle?.close()
        } catch {
            throw ZipError.writeFailed(error.localizedDescription)
        }
        handle = nil
        let manager = FileManager.default
        if manager.fileExists(atPath: destination.path) { try manager.removeItem(at: destination) }
        try manager.moveItem(at: partial, to: destination)
    }

    public mutating func cancel() {
        try? handle?.close()
        handle = nil
        try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: partial)
    }

    private mutating func flush() throws {
        guard !buffer.isEmpty else { return }
        do {
            try handle?.write(contentsOf: buffer)
        } catch {
            throw ZipError.writeFailed(error.localizedDescription)
        }
        buffer.removeAll(keepingCapacity: true)
    }
}
```

Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Export/ThreeMFText.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// The text of a 3MF model (implicit spec §5.3): numbers with six significant digits, written by hand because `FormatStyle` takes
/// microseconds a number and a part has millions, and names made safe for XML.
enum ThreeMFText {
    private static let powers: [Double] = (0...30).map { pow(10, Double($0)) }

    /// Appends `value` to six significant digits, as plain decimal text with no exponent and no trailing zeros ("0.000123457",
    /// "12.3457", "123457", "-5"). A value that is not finite is written as 0.
    static func append(_ value: Float, to bytes: inout [UInt8]) {
        let number = Double(value)
        guard number.isFinite, number != 0 else {
            bytes.append(UInt8(ascii: "0"))
            return
        }
        if number < 0 { bytes.append(UInt8(ascii: "-")) }
        let magnitude = abs(number)
        var exponent = Int(floor(log10(magnitude)))
        // Six digits: 100000 ... 999999.
        var digits = (magnitude * scale(5 - exponent)).rounded()
        if digits >= 1_000_000 {
            exponent += 1
            digits = (magnitude * scale(5 - exponent)).rounded()
        }
        var text = Array(String(Int(digits)).utf8)
        let pointAt = exponent + 1  // digits before the point
        if pointAt <= 0 {
            text = [UInt8(ascii: "0"), UInt8(ascii: ".")] + [UInt8](repeating: UInt8(ascii: "0"), count: -pointAt) + text
        } else if pointAt >= text.count {
            text += [UInt8](repeating: UInt8(ascii: "0"), count: pointAt - text.count)
        } else {
            text.insert(UInt8(ascii: "."), at: pointAt)
        }
        if text.contains(UInt8(ascii: ".")) {
            while text.last == UInt8(ascii: "0") { text.removeLast() }
            if text.last == UInt8(ascii: ".") { text.removeLast() }
        }
        bytes += text
    }

    /// 10^power, exactly for the powers in the table and by `pow` beyond.
    private static func scale(_ power: Int) -> Double {
        if power >= 0 { return power < powers.count ? powers[power] : pow(10, Double(power)) }
        return 1 / (-power < powers.count ? powers[-power] : pow(10, Double(-power)))
    }

    static func append(_ value: UInt32, to bytes: inout [UInt8]) {
        bytes += Array(String(value).utf8)
    }

    /// `name` for an XML attribute: the five characters XML reserves become entities, control characters go, and a blank name is "Part".
    static func attribute(_ name: String) -> String {
        var result = ""
        for character in name.unicodeScalars {
            switch character {
            case "&": result += "&amp;"
            case "<": result += "&lt;"
            case ">": result += "&gt;"
            case "\"": result += "&quot;"
            case "'": result += "&apos;"
            default:
                if character.value >= 0x20 && character.value != 0x7F { result.unicodeScalars.append(character) }
            }
        }
        return result.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces).isEmpty ? "Part" : result
    }
}
```

Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Export/ThreeMFWriter.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// Writes a 3MF (implicit spec §5.3): an OPC zip with `[Content_Types].xml`, `_rels/.rels` and `3D/3dmodel.model`, unit millimetre, one
/// `<object>` per object (the Output node's name) and a `<build>` item for each. The model's XML puts every vertex before any
/// triangle, but windows arrive with both, so each object's vertices and triangles are written to two temporary files as they come
/// and joined, entry by entry, into the zip when the file is finished (`ZipWriter`, deflated where it can be). Vertices have six
/// significant digits. Memory is the size of a window.
public struct ThreeMFWriter: MeshSink {
    private struct Object {
        var name: String
        var vertices: URL
        var triangles: URL
        var vertexHandle: FileHandle
        var triangleHandle: FileHandle
    }

    private let destination: URL
    private var finished: [Object] = []
    private var open: Object?
    private var vertexBuffer: [UInt8] = []
    private var triangleBuffer: [UInt8] = []
    public private(set) var triangleCount = 0
    public private(set) var vertexCount = 0

    public init(destination: URL) {
        self.destination = destination
    }

    public mutating func beginObject(name: String) throws {
        guard open == nil else { throw ZipError.misuse("the last object is still open") }
        let folder = FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory
        let stem = "mc-3mf-\(UUID().uuidString)"
        let (vertices, triangles) = (folder.appending(path: "\(stem).vertices"), folder.appending(path: "\(stem).triangles"))
        guard FileManager.default.createFile(atPath: vertices.path, contents: nil),
              FileManager.default.createFile(atPath: triangles.path, contents: nil) else {
            throw ZipError.writeFailed("the temporary folder can't be written to.")
        }
        open = Object(name: name, vertices: vertices, triangles: triangles, vertexHandle: try FileHandle(forWritingTo: vertices),
                      triangleHandle: try FileHandle(forWritingTo: triangles))
        vertexCount = 0
    }

    public mutating func add(_ window: MeshWindow) throws {
        guard open != nil else { throw ZipError.misuse("no object is open") }
        let first = window.firstNew
        for (local, global) in window.globalIndex.enumerated() where global >= first {
            let p = window.positions[local]
            vertexBuffer += Array("<vertex x=\"".utf8)
            ThreeMFText.append(p.x, to: &vertexBuffer)
            vertexBuffer += Array("\" y=\"".utf8)
            ThreeMFText.append(p.y, to: &vertexBuffer)
            vertexBuffer += Array("\" z=\"".utf8)
            ThreeMFText.append(p.z, to: &vertexBuffer)
            vertexBuffer += Array("\"/>\n".utf8)
            vertexCount += 1
        }
        for base in stride(from: 0, to: window.triangles.count - 2, by: 3) {
            triangleBuffer += Array("<triangle v1=\"".utf8)
            ThreeMFText.append(window.globalIndex[Int(window.triangles[base])], to: &triangleBuffer)
            triangleBuffer += Array("\" v2=\"".utf8)
            ThreeMFText.append(window.globalIndex[Int(window.triangles[base + 1])], to: &triangleBuffer)
            triangleBuffer += Array("\" v3=\"".utf8)
            ThreeMFText.append(window.globalIndex[Int(window.triangles[base + 2])], to: &triangleBuffer)
            triangleBuffer += Array("\"/>\n".utf8)
            triangleCount += 1
        }
        if vertexBuffer.count + triangleBuffer.count >= 1 << 20 { try flush() }
    }

    public mutating func endObject() throws {
        try flush()
        guard let object = open else { throw ZipError.misuse("no object is open") }
        try object.vertexHandle.close()
        try object.triangleHandle.close()
        finished.append(object)
        open = nil
    }

    public mutating func finish() throws {
        guard open == nil else { throw ZipError.misuse("the last object is still open") }
        defer { removeTemporaries() }
        var zip = try ZipWriter(destination: destination)
        do {
            try Self.write(&zip, "[Content_Types].xml", Self.contentTypes)
            try Self.write(&zip, "_rels/.rels", Self.relationships)
            try zip.beginEntry("3D/3dmodel.model")
            try zip.write(Array(Self.modelHeader.utf8))
            for (index, object) in finished.enumerated() {
                let name = ThreeMFText.attribute(object.name)
                try zip.write(Array("<object id=\"\(index + 1)\" type=\"model\" name=\"\(name)\"><mesh>\n<vertices>\n".utf8))
                try Self.copy(object.vertices, into: &zip)
                try zip.write(Array("</vertices>\n<triangles>\n".utf8))
                try Self.copy(object.triangles, into: &zip)
                try zip.write(Array("</triangles>\n</mesh></object>\n".utf8))
            }
            let items = finished.indices.map { "<item objectid=\"\($0 + 1)\"/>" }.joined()
            try zip.write(Array("</resources>\n<build>\(items)</build>\n</model>\n".utf8))
            try zip.endEntry()
            try zip.finish()
        } catch {
            zip.cancel()
            throw error
        }
    }

    public mutating func cancel() {
        if let object = open {
            try? object.vertexHandle.close()
            try? object.triangleHandle.close()
            finished.append(object)
            open = nil
        }
        removeTemporaries()
    }

    // MARK: Pieces

    private mutating func flush() throws {
        guard let object = open else { return }
        do {
            try object.vertexHandle.write(contentsOf: vertexBuffer)
            try object.triangleHandle.write(contentsOf: triangleBuffer)
        } catch {
            throw ZipError.writeFailed(error.localizedDescription)
        }
        vertexBuffer.removeAll(keepingCapacity: true)
        triangleBuffer.removeAll(keepingCapacity: true)
    }

    private mutating func removeTemporaries() {
        for object in finished {
            try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: object.vertices)
            try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: object.triangles)
        }
        finished = []
    }

    private static func write(_ zip: inout ZipWriter, _ name: String, _ text: String) throws {
        try zip.beginEntry(name)
        try zip.write(Array(text.utf8))
        try zip.endEntry()
    }

    /// Streams a temporary file into the open entry, a megabyte at a time.
    private static func copy(_ url: URL, into zip: inout ZipWriter) throws {
        let reader = try FileHandle(forReadingFrom: url)
        defer { try? reader.close() }
        while let chunk = try reader.read(upToCount: 1 << 20), !chunk.isEmpty { try zip.write(Array(chunk)) }
    }

    static let contentTypes = """
        <?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
        <Types xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/content-types">\
        <Default Extension="rels" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-package.relationships+xml"/>\
        <Default Extension="model" ContentType="application/vnd.ms-package.3dmanufacturing-3dmodel+xml"/></Types>

        """

    static let relationships = """
        <?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
        <Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships">\
        <Relationship Target="/3D/3dmodel.model" Id="rel0" Type="http://schemas.microsoft.com/3dmanufacturing/2013/01/3dmodel"/>\
        </Relationships>

        """

    static let modelHeader = """
        <?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
        <model unit="millimeter" xml:lang="en-US" xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/3dmanufacturing/core/2015/02">
        <resources>

        """
}
```

### Task 8: The streaming mesher (spec Errata D12)

Errata D12: *bricks are meshed in a fixed order, and each brick's triangles go straight to the STL or 3MF writer. Shared-edge vertices are merged
within a window of neighbouring bricks, so the file stays watertight. Decimate collapses inside each brick and never moves a brick-boundary vertex.*

**How it works**, on 7c-core's real names (`BrickPlan`, `MeshBuilder`, `ImplicitMesher.snap`):

- `BrickPlan.build` is the one place that is not streamed: the surviving bricks (16 bytes each; a 100 mm gyroid at 0.1 mm has some hundred
  thousand) are held, sorted by x, then y, then z. That is the plan, not the mesh.
- A **window** is a run of whole brick columns (all bricks of one brick-x) until `windowBricks` (default 20,000) is reached. Each window is meshed
  by a fresh `MeshBuilder` (its vertex table is the window's, not the whole mesh's) and snapped by `ImplicitMesher.snap`.
- **Sharing.** Two windows share exactly the lattice plane between them. A vertex on it is made twice, once by each window, from the same samples
  with the same arithmetic, so its position has the **same bits** both times. The window keeps the vertices within 1.5 voxels of its top plane
  (the snap moves a vertex at most half a voxel), the next window finds its own by their bits, and takes the earlier window's number. So a shared
  vertex is written once, and the sink never holds more than the window and one plane. (If a future GPU path made two windows disagree in the last
  bit, the vertices would be written twice and an STL would still be watertight by coordinates; a 3MF would not be. 7c-1g's agreement tests must
  keep windows bit-stable; see the shared-files table.)
- **Decimate per window** (`MeshStage.decimate`): `MeshDecimator` pins the window's open edge, which is exactly the shared plane, so its vertices and
  edges come out as they went in and the windows still fit; the carry then matches them by bits as before. The test streams a box in several windows
  with a stage and requires one closed manifold of Euler characteristic 2.
- **No memory refusal.** `MeshingBudget.check` (the whole mesh in memory) does not apply; Task 9 checks that the *plan* fits.
- `ImplicitMesher.snap` goes from `private` to internal (the one edit to 7c-core's mesher).

**Files:**
- Create: `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Meshing/MeshStage.swift`, `StreamingMesher.swift`; `Tests/CreatorImplicitTests/StreamingMesherTests.swift`, `Support/CollectingSink.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Meshing/ImplicitMesher.swift` (`snap` internal)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 7's `MeshSink`/`MeshWindow`/`ThreeMFWriter`/`ZipReader`; Task 1's `MeshDecimator`; `BrickPlan`, `MeshBuilder`, `ImplicitMesher.snap`.
- Produces: `StreamingMesher(evaluator:windowBricks:bricksPerBatch:)` with
  `stream(_:resolution:stages:startingAt:into:progress:) async throws -> Report(windows, vertices, triangles, largestWindow)`;
  `MeshStage.decimate(tolerance:)`.

Create `Tests/CreatorImplicitTests/Support/CollectingSink.swift`:

```swift
import CreatorImplicit

/// A `MeshSink` that puts the windows back together, for the tests of the streamed export: the whole mesh by global vertex number.
struct CollectingSink: MeshSink {
    private(set) var positions: [SIMD3<Float>] = []
    private(set) var triangles: [UInt32] = []
    private(set) var windows = 0
    private(set) var objects: [String] = []
    private(set) var isFinished = false
    private(set) var isCancelled = false
    var triangleCount: Int { triangles.count / 3 }
    var vertexCount: Int { positions.count }

    mutating func beginObject(name: String) throws {
        objects.append(name)
    }

    mutating func add(_ window: MeshWindow) throws {
        windows += 1
        for (local, global) in window.globalIndex.enumerated() where global >= window.firstNew {
            precondition(Int(global) == positions.count, "new vertices come in order")
            positions.append(window.positions[local])
        }
        triangles += window.triangles.map { window.globalIndex[Int($0)] }
    }

    mutating func endObject() throws {}

    mutating func finish() throws {
        isFinished = true
    }

    mutating func cancel() {
        isCancelled = true
    }

    /// The mesh the windows made.
    var mesh: TriangleMesh { TriangleMesh(positions: positions, triangles: triangles) }
}
```

Create `Tests/CreatorImplicitTests/StreamingMesherTests.swift`:

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import Foundation
import Synchronization
import Testing
@testable import CreatorImplicit

/// The streamed export's mesher (implicit spec Errata D12, §7): window by window it makes the mesh the whole-mesh mesher makes, never
/// holding more than a window.
struct StreamingMesherTests {
    func streamer(_ solid: ImplicitSolid, windowBricks: Int = 6) -> StreamingMesher {
        StreamingMesher(evaluator: CPUBatchEvaluator(solid), windowBricks: windowBricks, bricksPerBatch: 5)
    }

    func triangleSet(_ mesh: TriangleMesh) -> Set<[SIMD3<Float>]> {
        Set((0..<mesh.triangleCount).map { index in
            let corners = (0..<3).map { mesh.positions[Int(mesh.triangles[3 * index + $0])] }
            // The same triangle from any corner: start at the smallest.
            let keys = corners.map { [$0.x, $0.y, $0.z] }
            let start = (0..<3).min { keys[$0].lexicographicallyPrecedes(keys[$1]) } ?? 0
            return [corners[start], corners[(start + 1) % 3], corners[(start + 2) % 3]]
        })
    }

    @Test func aSphereInManyWindowsIsTheMeshTheWholeMesherMakes() async throws {
        let solid = ImplicitSolid.sphere(radius: 10)
        var sink = CollectingSink()
        let report = try await streamer(solid).stream(solid, resolution: 0.5, into: &sink)
        let whole = try await ImplicitMesher(evaluator: CPUBatchEvaluator(solid)).mesh(solid, resolution: 0.5, quality: .export)
        #expect(report.windows >= 4 && sink.windows == report.windows, "\(report.windows) windows")
        let streamed = sink.mesh
        #expect(streamed.vertexCount == whole.vertexCount && streamed.triangleCount == whole.triangleCount)
        #expect(triangleSet(streamed) == triangleSet(whole), "the very same triangles")
        let topology = MeshTopology(streamed)
        #expect(topology.isClosedManifold && topology.eulerCharacteristic == 2)
        #expect(report.vertices == whole.vertexCount && report.triangles == whole.triangleCount)
        #expect(report.largestWindow < whole.triangleCount / 2, "memory is a window, not the mesh")
    }

    @Test func aRingAndTwoFarPiecesStreamClosed() async throws {
        let ring = ImplicitSolid.subtract([.cylinder(radius: 8, halfHeight: 3), .cylinder(radius: 4, halfHeight: 5)], blend: .constant(0))
        var sink = CollectingSink()
        _ = try await streamer(ring).stream(ring, resolution: 0.5, into: &sink)
        #expect(MeshTopology(sink.mesh).isClosedManifold && MeshTopology(sink.mesh).eulerCharacteristic == 0)
        let far = ImplicitSolid.moved(.sphere(radius: 3), RigidMove(origin: Vector3(30, 0, 0), xAxis: .unitX, yAxis: .unitY, zAxis: .unitZ))
        let pair = ImplicitSolid.union([.sphere(radius: 3), far], blend: .constant(0))
        var apart = CollectingSink()
        _ = try await streamer(pair, windowBricks: 2).stream(pair, resolution: 0.4, into: &apart)
        #expect(MeshTopology(apart.mesh).isClosedManifold && MeshTopology(apart.mesh).eulerCharacteristic == 4)
    }

    @Test func theWindowSizeChangesNothingInTheOutput() async throws {
        let solid = ImplicitFixtures.cylinder
        var sets: [Set<[SIMD3<Float>]>] = []
        for size in [3, 7, 1_000_000] {
            var sink = CollectingSink()
            _ = try await streamer(solid, windowBricks: size).stream(solid, resolution: 0.5, into: &sink)
            sets.append(triangleSet(sink.mesh))
        }
        #expect(sets[0] == sets[1] && sets[1] == sets[2])
    }

    @Test func decimatingEachWindowKeepsTheWholeClosedAndTheShapeWithinTheTolerance() async throws {
        let solid = ImplicitSolid.box(halfSize: Vector3(6, 4, 3), cornerRadius: 0)
        var plain = CollectingSink()
        _ = try await streamer(solid, windowBricks: 4).stream(solid, resolution: 0.4, into: &plain)
        var slim = CollectingSink()
        let report = try await streamer(solid, windowBricks: 4).stream(solid, resolution: 0.4, stages: [.decimate(tolerance: 0.02)],
                                                                         into: &slim)
        #expect(report.windows >= 3)
        let topology = MeshTopology(slim.mesh)
        #expect(topology.isClosedManifold && topology.eulerCharacteristic == 2, "\(topology)")
        #expect(slim.mesh.triangleCount < plain.mesh.triangleCount / 3, "\(plain.mesh.triangleCount) → \(slim.mesh.triangleCount)")
        #expect(abs(slim.mesh.signedVolume - plain.mesh.signedVolume) / plain.mesh.signedVolume < 0.002, "the volume hardly moves")
        #expect(report.triangles == slim.mesh.triangleCount && report.vertices == slim.mesh.vertexCount)
    }

    @Test func aCancelStopsBetweenWindowsAndTheSinkIsLeftForTheCallerToCancel() async {
        let solid = ImplicitSolid.sphere(radius: 10)
        let task = Task {
            var sink = CollectingSink()
            let seen = Mutex(0)
            do {
                _ = try await streamer(solid, windowBricks: 4).stream(solid, resolution: 0.5, into: &sink) { fraction in
                    if fraction > 0.2 { seen.withLock { $0 += 1 } }
                    if fraction > 0.2 { withUnsafeCurrentTask { $0?.cancel() } }
                }
                return (false, sink.windows)
            } catch is CancellationError {
                return (true, sink.windows)
            } catch {
                return (false, -1)
            }
        }
        let (cancelled, windows) = await task.value
        #expect(cancelled && windows >= 1 && windows < 20)
    }

    @Test func aSecondMeshInTheSameObjectContinuesTheNumbering() async throws {
        let first = ImplicitSolid.sphere(radius: 4)
        let second = ImplicitSolid.sphere(radius: 3)
        var sink = CollectingSink()
        let one = try await streamer(first).stream(first, resolution: 0.5, into: &sink)
        let two = try await streamer(second).stream(second, resolution: 0.5, startingAt: sink.vertexCount, into: &sink)
        #expect(one.vertices + two.vertices == sink.vertexCount)
        #expect(MeshTopology(sink.mesh).isClosedManifold && MeshTopology(sink.mesh).eulerCharacteristic == 4, "two closed shells")
    }

    @Test func aSolidWithNoSurfaceIsRefused() async {
        let solid = ImplicitSolid.sphere(radius: 0.01)
        var sink = CollectingSink()
        await #expect(throws: ImplicitMeshingError.empty) { try await streamer(solid).stream(solid, resolution: 0.5, into: &sink) }
    }

    @Test func progressRunsFromZeroToOne() async throws {
        let solid = ImplicitSolid.sphere(radius: 6)
        var sink = CollectingSink()
        let seen = Mutex<[Double]>([])
        _ = try await streamer(solid, windowBricks: 4).stream(solid, resolution: 0.5, into: &sink) { value in
            seen.withLock { $0.append(value) }
        }
        let values = seen.withLock { $0 }
        #expect(values.first == 0 && values.last == 1 && values == values.sorted() && values.count > 3)
    }

    @Test func aStreamToAFileHoldsTheSameTrianglesAsTheMesh() async throws {
        let solid = ImplicitSolid.sphere(radius: 6)
        let url = URL.temporaryDirectory.appending(path: "stream-\(UUID().uuidString).3mf")
        defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) }
        var writer = ThreeMFWriter(destination: url)
        try writer.beginObject(name: "Ball")
        _ = try await streamer(solid, windowBricks: 4).stream(solid, resolution: 0.5, into: &writer)
        try writer.endObject()
        try writer.finish()
        let model = try #require(ThreeMFReading.read(try ZipReader(contentsOf: url).contents(of: "3D/3dmodel.model")))
        let object = try #require(model.objects.first)
        let whole = try await ImplicitMesher(evaluator: CPUBatchEvaluator(solid)).mesh(solid, resolution: 0.5, quality: .export)
        #expect(object.vertices.count == whole.vertexCount && object.triangles.count == whole.triangleCount)
        let mesh = TriangleMesh(positions: object.vertices.map { SIMD3(Float($0.x), Float($0.y), Float($0.z)) },
                                triangles: object.triangles.flatMap { $0.map { UInt32($0) } })
        #expect(MeshTopology(mesh).isClosedManifold && MeshTopology(mesh).eulerCharacteristic == 2)
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Meshing/MeshStage.swift`:

```swift
/// A step a streamed export applies to every window on its way to the file (implicit spec Errata D12). Decimate is the only one: a
/// check does not change the surface, so it runs on a copy (`MeshCopyForChecks`), not on the stream.
public enum MeshStage: Hashable, Sendable {
    /// Quadric error decimation of each window with the window's edge held still, so the pieces still fit.
    case decimate(tolerance: Double)
}
```

Create `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Meshing/StreamingMesher.swift`:

```swift
import CreatorGeometry

/// Meshes an implicit solid straight into a file (implicit spec Errata D12): the bricks the surface passes through are taken in order,
/// a run of whole columns of them at a time (a window), each window is meshed and snapped on its own and handed to a `MeshSink`,
/// and nothing of it is kept but the vertices on the edge it shares with the next window. A whole 0.1 mm lattice is some 150 million
/// triangles; this holds one window of them. The vertices both windows make on the shared plane have the same bits (the samples
/// there are the same numbers, the marching cubes rule and the snap are the same arithmetic), so they are matched by bits and
/// written once, and the file is as watertight as a mesh made whole (`StreamingMesherTests` holds it to the same triangles).
///
/// Each window may be decimated first (`MeshStage.decimate`). A window's open edge is the plane it shares with its neighbour; the
/// decimator pins every vertex on an open edge, so the plane's vertices and edges come out exactly as they went in.
public struct StreamingMesher: Sendable {
    public struct Report: Sendable {
        public var windows = 0
        public var vertices = 0
        public var triangles = 0
        /// The most triangles one window held: what memory scales with.
        public var largestWindow = 0
    }

    public let evaluator: any ImplicitBatchEvaluator
    /// About how many bricks a window holds: whole columns are taken until there are at least this many.
    public let windowBricks: Int
    /// How many bricks are sampled at a time inside a window.
    public let bricksPerBatch: Int

    public init(evaluator: any ImplicitBatchEvaluator, windowBricks: Int = 20_000, bricksPerBatch: Int = 4096) {
        self.evaluator = evaluator
        self.windowBricks = max(1, windowBricks)
        self.bricksPerBatch = max(1, bricksPerBatch)
    }

    /// The vertex a window shares with its neighbour: its bits are its identity.
    private struct VertexKey: Hashable {
        var x: UInt32
        var y: UInt32
        var z: UInt32

        init(_ position: SIMD3<Float>) {
            (x, y, z) = (position.x.bitPattern, position.y.bitPattern, position.z.bitPattern)
        }
    }

    /// Meshes `solid` with voxels of `resolution` millimetres into `sink`, which the caller has begun an object on and will end and
    /// finish. There is no memory refusal: the window is the memory.
    /// - Parameters:
    ///   - stages: applied to each window in order.
    ///   - firstVertex: the number the first new vertex gets: how many vertices the sink's object already holds, when this is not the
    ///     first mesh in it.
    ///   - progress: the fraction of bricks done, 0 to 1.
    /// - Throws: `ImplicitMeshingError` and `CancellationError`.
    public func stream(_ solid: ImplicitSolid, resolution: Double, stages: [MeshStage] = [], startingAt firstVertex: Int = 0,
                       into sink: inout some MeshSink, progress: @Sendable (Double) -> Void = { _ in }) async throws -> Report {
        progress(0)
        let plan = try await BrickPlan.build(for: solid, resolution: resolution, using: evaluator)
        guard !plan.bricks.isEmpty else { throw ImplicitMeshingError.empty }
        var report = Report()
        var carried: [VertexKey: UInt32] = [:]
        var carriedPlane: Int32?
        var done = 0
        for range in windows(of: plan.bricks) {
            try Task.checkCancellation()
            let bricks = plan.bricks[range]
            var (positions, triangles) = try await mesh(bricks, of: plan)
            for stage in stages {
                switch stage {
                case .decimate(let tolerance):
                    let output = try MeshDecimator.decimate(positions: positions, triangles: triangles, tolerance: tolerance)
                    (positions, triangles) = (output.positions, output.triangles)
                }
            }
            let bottom = Int32(bricks.first?.x ?? 0)
            guard !triangles.isEmpty else {
                done += bricks.count
                continue
            }
            let sharing = carriedPlane == bottom ? carried : [:]
            let first = UInt32(firstVertex + report.vertices)
            let window = Self.window(positions: positions, triangles: triangles, matching: sharing, firstNew: first)
            try sink.add(window)
            report.windows += 1
            report.vertices += positions.count - window.globalIndex.filter { $0 < window.firstNew }.count
            report.triangles += window.triangleCount
            report.largestWindow = max(report.largestWindow, window.triangleCount)
            // What the next window may share: the vertices near this one's top plane.
            let top = Int32(bricks.last?.x ?? 0) + 1
            let topX = Float(plan.point(Int(top) * BrickPlan.brickSize, 0, 0).x - 1.5 * resolution)
            carried = [:]
            for (index, position) in window.positions.enumerated() where position.x >= topX {
                carried[VertexKey(position)] = window.globalIndex[index]
            }
            carriedPlane = top
            done += bricks.count
            progress(Double(done) / Double(plan.bricks.count))
        }
        guard report.triangles > 0 else { throw ImplicitMeshingError.empty }
        return report
    }

    /// The runs of whole brick columns (all bricks of one x) that make up the windows, as ranges of the plan's bricks.
    func windows(of bricks: [SIMD3<Int32>]) -> [Range<Int>] {
        var result: [Range<Int>] = []
        var start = 0
        while start < bricks.count {
            var end = start
            while end < bricks.count {
                let column = bricks[end].x
                while end < bricks.count, bricks[end].x == column { end += 1 }
                if end - start >= windowBricks { break }
            }
            result.append(start..<end)
            start = end
        }
        return result
    }

    /// The marching cubes mesh of `bricks`, its vertices snapped to the surface.
    private func mesh(_ bricks: ArraySlice<SIMD3<Int32>>, of plan: BrickPlan) async throws -> ([SIMD3<Float>], [UInt32]) {
        var builder = MeshBuilder(plan: plan)
        for start in stride(from: bricks.startIndex, to: bricks.endIndex, by: bricksPerBatch) {
            try Task.checkCancellation()
            let batch = bricks[start..<min(start + bricksPerBatch, bricks.endIndex)]
            builder.add(bricks: batch, samples: try await plan.samples(of: batch, using: evaluator))
        }
        var positions = builder.positions
        var normals = [SIMD3<Float>](repeating: .zero, count: positions.count)
        try await ImplicitMesher.snap(&positions, &normals, resolution: plan.resolution, evaluator: evaluator) { _ in }
        return (positions, builder.triangles)
    }

    /// The window with its vertices numbered: one that the neighbour already wrote takes the neighbour's number, the others count on
    /// from `firstNew`.
    private static func window(positions: [SIMD3<Float>], triangles: [UInt32], matching shared: [VertexKey: UInt32],
                               firstNew: UInt32) -> MeshWindow {
        var next = firstNew
        var numbers: [UInt32] = []
        numbers.reserveCapacity(positions.count)
        for position in positions {
            if !shared.isEmpty, let known = shared[VertexKey(position)] {
                numbers.append(known)
            } else {
                numbers.append(next)
                next += 1
            }
        }
        return MeshWindow(positions: positions, triangles: triangles, globalIndex: numbers, firstNew: firstNew)
    }
}
```

### Task 9: The streamed export through the graph, and File ▸ Export 3MF…

Errata D12 *Decimate and the checks run during export on that stream per brick: ... Otherwise they run on an in-memory copy at preview resolution,
which is labelled "approximate (preview resolution)".* This task makes the graph hand the writer a stream instead of a mesh, and the app write it.

**The mechanism.** `MeshQuality` gains `.streamedExport`. A run of the graph with it (only `AppModel.export` makes one; every existing run, test and
the viewport use `.preview` or `.export` and are untouched) makes the **Mesh node return a `MeshStream`**: the implicit, the resolution, no stages.
`MeshStream` is a second `MeshPayload` beside `TriangleMesh` (triangle count 0: unknown until written). Every node that takes a mesh socket now
declares `readsMeshQuality` (an implicit wired straight in is meshed by *it*, so its result depends on the run) and handles both:

| Node | `TriangleMesh` (preview, in-memory export) | `MeshStream` (streamed export) |
|---|---|---|
| Mesh | meshes (unchanged) | hands over the stream; refuses a plan that cannot fit (`MeshingBudget.check` with 256 bytes a brick, not the 12,800 of a mesh in memory) with the same words as before ("This needs about N GB at 0.1 mm; …") |
| Output | passes meshes through | passes streams through; an implicit wired in straight becomes a stream at the Mesh node's defaults |
| Decimate | decimates | **adds a stage**: each window is decimated as it is written (exact, Task 8) |
| Check Thin Walls, Check Trapped Volumes | check the mesh, flag it | check a **copy** (`MeshStream.checkCopy`: the stream's solid meshed in memory at the export resolution when that is at most 2,000,000 triangles, else as much coarser as brings it there) and pass the stream on unchanged; the warning says "(approximate: coarser than the export)" only when the copy was coarser |
| Mesh to Solid | cap, weld, kernel | **meshes the stream into memory with its stages applied** (`MeshStream.materialized`); past 100,000 triangles it counts them (without keeping any) and gives the spec's message with the true N, so Decimate first is how a big part gets in |

`StreamedExport` (CreatorApp) is the writer: each Output node is a `beginObject(name)`…`endObject()`; its solids are tessellated by the kernel
(0.01 mm) and written; its `TriangleMesh`es are written as they are; each `MeshStream` goes through `StreamingMesher` on the engine the stream's solid
compiles for (Metal where it can, else the CPU); progress is the streams done out of all. A cancel (the sheet's) or any error calls the sink's `cancel()`,
which removes the half-written file and the temporaries. **`ExportKind`** (`step`, `stl`, `3mf`) replaces the kernel's `ExportFormat` in `AppModel`'s
API (`.step` and `.stl` still read the same at every call site); STEP and STL of solids alone stay the kernel's own writers; anything with a mesh in
it, and every 3MF, is streamed. STEP still refuses a mesh with the spec's message, which already says "Export STL or 3MF".

**The menu.** File ▸ Export 3MF… (no shortcut: ⌘E and ⇧⌘E are taken), and nowhere else: the spec names only the File menu, so the top bar's Export menu stays STEP and STL (decision 9). The save
panel offers `Insole.3mf` and filters by `.3mf` (`ContentType.threeMF`).

**Files:**
- Create: `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Mesh/MeshStream.swift`, `Sources/CreatorImplicit/Export/MeshCollector.swift` (also `MeshCounter`),
  `Sources/CreatorNodes/Implicit/MeshTools/MeshValue.swift`, `Sources/CreatorApp/Export/ExportKind.swift`, `StreamedExport.swift`;
  `Tests/CreatorImplicitTests/MeshStreamTests.swift`, `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Implicit/StreamedMeshNodeTests.swift`, `Support/StreamedExportSupport.swift`,
  `Tests/CreatorAppTests/ExportStreamedTests.swift`
- Replace whole: `Sources/CreatorNodes/Implicit/MeshInput.swift`, `MeshTools/DecimateNode.swift`, `CheckThinWallsNode.swift`, `CheckTrappedVolumesNode.swift`,

- Modify: `MeshQuality.swift`, `MeshNode.swift`, `OutputNode.swift`, `CheckWording.swift`, `MeshingBudget.swift`, `ContentType+MetalCreator.swift`,
  `ContentTypeTests.swift`, `AppCommands.swift`, `ExportPlan.swift`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 1 to 8.
- Produces: `MeshQuality.streamedExport`; `MeshStream(solid:resolution:stages:)` (`appending(_:)`, `materialized(limit:using:progress:)`,
  `checkCopy(using:triangleBudget:)`, `Failure.tooManyTriangles(Int)`); `MeshCollector`, `MeshCounter`; `MeshInput.payloads/value/checkable/collected`;
  `ExportKind` (`.step/.stl/.threeMF`, `title`, `kernelFormat`); `AppModel.export(_:to:)` and `exportDocument(_:using:)` over `ExportKind`;
  `ContentType.threeMF`, `ContentType.exported(_ kind:)`; `MeshingBudget.streamedBytesPerBrick`.

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import Testing
@testable import CreatorImplicit

/// The stream a Mesh node hands over while an export streams (implicit spec Errata D12).
struct MeshStreamTests {
    let ball = ImplicitSolid.sphere(radius: 5)

    @Test func aStreamIsNothingMeshedYetAndSaysSo() {
        let stream = MeshStream(solid: ball, resolution: 0.1)
        #expect(stream.triangleCount == 0 && stream.estimatedBytes < 1_000)
        #expect(stream.inspectorFacts == [PayloadFact("Export", "Streamed to the file at 0.1 mm")])
        let slim = stream.appending(.decimate(tolerance: 0.02))
        #expect(slim.stages == [.decimate(tolerance: 0.02)] && stream.stages.isEmpty, "appending makes a new stream")
        #expect(slim.inspectorFacts.last == PayloadFact("Decimate", "within 0.02 mm"))
    }

    @Test func materializedIsTheMeshWithItsStagesApplied() async throws {
        let stream = MeshStream(solid: ball, resolution: 0.4)
        let plain = try await stream.materialized(limit: 100_000, using: CPUBatchEvaluator(ball))
        #expect(MeshTopology(plain).isClosedManifold && plain.quality == .export && plain.resolution == 0.4)
        let slim = try await stream.appending(.decimate(tolerance: 0.05)).materialized(limit: 100_000, using: CPUBatchEvaluator(ball))
        #expect(MeshTopology(slim).isClosedManifold && slim.triangleCount < plain.triangleCount)
    }

    @Test func pastTheLimitItSaysHowManyTrianglesThereAre() async throws {
        let stream = MeshStream(solid: ball, resolution: 0.4)
        let all = try await stream.materialized(limit: .max, using: CPUBatchEvaluator(ball))
        await #expect(throws: MeshStream.Failure.tooManyTriangles(all.triangleCount)) {
            try await stream.materialized(limit: 100, using: CPUBatchEvaluator(ball))
        }
    }

    @Test func aCheckCopyIsAtTheStreamsResolutionWhenItFits() async throws {
        let stream = MeshStream(solid: ball, resolution: 0.4)
        let copy = try await stream.checkCopy(using: CPUBatchEvaluator(ball))
        #expect(copy.isExact && MeshTopology(copy.mesh).isClosedManifold && copy.mesh.resolution == 0.4)
    }

    @Test func aCheckCopyOfAPartThatIsTooBigIsCoarserAndSaysSo() async throws {
        let stream = MeshStream(solid: ball, resolution: 0.1)
        let copy = try await stream.checkCopy(using: CPUBatchEvaluator(ball), triangleBudget: 3_000)
        #expect(!copy.isExact && copy.mesh.resolution > 0.1)
        #expect(copy.mesh.triangleCount < 12_000, "about the budget, not the 120,000 an exact copy needs")
    }

    @Test func aStreamOfNothingIsRefused() async {
        let tiny = ImplicitSolid.sphere(radius: 0.01)
        await #expect(throws: ImplicitMeshingError.empty) {
            try await MeshStream(solid: tiny, resolution: 0.5).materialized(limit: 10, using: CPUBatchEvaluator(tiny))
        }
    }

    @Test func theCollectorAndTheCounterTakeWindows() throws {
        var collector = MeshCollector(limit: 20)
        let cube = MeshFixtures.cube
        try collector.add(MeshWindow(positions: cube.positions, triangles: cube.triangles, startingAt: 0))
        #expect(collector.triangleCount == 12 && collector.vertexCount == 8)
        #expect(throws: MeshCollector.Overflow()) {
            try collector.add(MeshWindow(positions: cube.positions, triangles: cube.triangles, startingAt: 8))
        }
        var counter = MeshCounter()
        try counter.add(MeshWindow(positions: cube.positions, triangles: cube.triangles, startingAt: 0))
        try counter.add(MeshWindow(positions: cube.positions, triangles: cube.triangles, startingAt: 8))
        #expect(counter.triangleCount == 24 && counter.vertexCount == 16)
    }
}
```

Create `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Support/StreamedExportSupport.swift`:

```swift
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import CreatorKernel
@testable import CreatorNodes

extension Harness {
    /// A run of the graph for an export that streams: Mesh nodes hand over streams instead of meshes.
    func runStreamed(_ demand: [Node], kernel: any Kernel = FakeKernel()) async throws -> EvaluationReport {
        let evaluator = Evaluator(registry: BuiltInNodes.registry, kernel: kernel)
        return try await evaluator.evaluate(graph, demand: Set(demand.map(\.id)), meshQuality: .streamedExport)
    }
}

extension EvaluationReport {
    /// The one stream on `socket` of `node`.
    func meshStream(_ node: Node, _ socket: SocketName = "mesh") -> MeshStream? {
        guard let items = value(node, socket)?.items, items.count == 1, case .mesh(let payload) = items[0] else { return nil }
        return payload as? MeshStream
    }
}
```

Create `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Implicit/StreamedMeshNodeTests.swift`:

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import CreatorKernel
import Testing
@testable import CreatorNodes
#if canImport(CreatorOCCT)
import CreatorOCCT
#endif

/// The mesh nodes while an export streams (implicit spec Errata D12): the Mesh node hands over a stream, Decimate adds a stage, the
/// checks look at a copy and let the stream through, Mesh to Solid meshes the stream into memory.
struct StreamedMeshNodeTests {
    /// A hollow ball (a shell of 0.3 mm), through Mesh at 0.1 mm, `tools` after it.
    func hollowBall(_ h: inout Harness) -> (shell: Node, mesh: Node) {
        let sphere = h.add(ImplicitSphereNode.self, ["radius": .number(2)])
        let shell = h.add(ImplicitShellNode.self, ["thickness": .number(0.3)])
        let mesh = h.add(MeshNode.self, ["export resolution": .number(0.1)])
        h.wire(sphere, "implicit", to: shell, "implicit")
        h.wire(shell, "implicit", to: mesh, "implicit")
        return (shell, mesh)
    }

    @Test func theMeshNodeHandsOverAStreamAtTheExportResolution() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let (shell, mesh) = hollowBall(&h)
        let report = try await h.runStreamed([mesh])
        let stream = try #require(report.meshStream(mesh))
        #expect(stream.resolution == 0.1 && stream.stages.isEmpty && stream.solid == report.implicitSolid(shell))
        #expect(report.isOK(mesh))
    }

    @Test func aPreviewAndAnInMemoryExportAreNotStreams() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let (_, mesh) = hollowBall(&h)
        #expect(try await h.run([mesh]).triangleMesh(mesh) != nil)
        #expect(try await h.runForExport([mesh]).triangleMesh(mesh)?.quality == .export)
    }

    @Test func outputPassesAStreamAndMakesOneOfAnImplicitWiredStraightIn() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let (_, mesh) = hollowBall(&h)
        let sphere = h.add(ImplicitSphereNode.self, ["radius": .number(1)])
        let passed = h.add(OutputNode.self)
        let made = h.add(OutputNode.self)
        h.wire(mesh, "mesh", to: passed, "mesh")
        h.wire(sphere, "implicit", to: made, "mesh")
        let report = try await h.runStreamed([passed, made])
        #expect(report.meshStream(passed)?.resolution == 0.1)
        #expect(report.meshStream(made)?.resolution == MeshNode.defaultExportResolution, "meshed at the defaults, as a stream")
    }

    @Test func decimateAddsAStageAndTwoAddTwo() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let (_, mesh) = hollowBall(&h)
        let first = h.add(DecimateNode.self, ["tolerance": .number(0.02)])
        let second = h.add(DecimateNode.self, ["tolerance": .number(0.05)])
        h.wire(mesh, "mesh", to: first, "mesh")
        h.wire(first, "mesh", to: second, "mesh")
        let report = try await h.runStreamed([second])

        let check = h.add(CheckTrappedVolumesNode.self)

    @Test func meshToSolidTakesAStreamWithItsDecimationApplied() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let box = h.add(ImplicitBoxNode.self, ["size": .vector(Vector3(8, 6, 4))])
        let mesh = h.add(MeshNode.self, ["export resolution": .number(0.25)])
        let decimate = h.add(DecimateNode.self, ["tolerance": .number(0.02)])
        let solid = h.add(MeshToSolidNode.self)
        h.wire(box, "implicit", to: mesh, "implicit")
        h.wire(mesh, "mesh", to: decimate, "mesh")
        h.wire(decimate, "mesh", to: solid, "mesh")
        let kernel = OCCTKernel()
        let report = try await h.runStreamed([solid], kernel: kernel)
        #expect(report.isOK(solid), "\(String(describing: report.state(solid)))")
        let made = try onlySolid(report, solid)
        let found = try await volume(made, kernel)
        #expect(abs(found - 192) / 192 < 0.02, "\(found) against 192")
        #expect(made.topology.faces.count < 200, "decimated, so few facets")
    }
    #endif
}
```

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorImplicit
import CreatorKernel
import CreatorNodes
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import CreatorApp

/// The streamed export (implicit spec §5.3, Errata D12): 3MF and STL written window by window, one 3MF object per Output node.
@MainActor
struct ExportStreamedTests {
    /// A folder of its own, removed with the test, so a stray temporary shows.
    final class Folder {
        let url = URL.temporaryDirectory.appending(path: "stream-export-\(UUID().uuidString)")

        init() {
            try? FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: url, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        }

        deinit {
            try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url)
        }

        func file(_ name: String) -> URL { url.appending(path: name) }

        var contents: [String] { ((try? FileManager.default.contentsOfDirectory(atPath: url.path)) ?? []).sorted() }
    }

    /// Sphere → Mesh (export resolution `export`) → [Decimate] → Output named `name`.
    func sphereGraph(radius: Double = 3, export: Double = 0.5, name: String = "Insole", decimate: Double? = nil,
                     into builder: inout GraphBuilder) -> Node {
        let sphere = builder.add(ImplicitSphereNode.self, ["radius": .number(radius)])
        let mesh = builder.add(MeshNode.self, ["export resolution": .number(export)])
        var output = builder.add(OutputNode.self)
        builder.wire(sphere, "implicit", to: mesh, "implicit")
        if let decimate {
            let node = builder.add(DecimateNode.self, ["tolerance": .number(decimate)])
            builder.wire(mesh, "mesh", to: node, "mesh")
            builder.wire(node, "mesh", to: output, "mesh")
        } else {
            builder.wire(mesh, "mesh", to: output, "mesh")
        }
        output.name = name
        builder.replace(output)
        return output
    }

    /// The model part of a 3MF: each object's name, vertices and triangles, found by pattern.
    func objects(_ url: URL) throws -> [(name: String, mesh: TriangleMesh)] {
        let model = String(bytes: try ZipReader(contentsOf: url).contents(of: "3D/3dmodel.model"), encoding: .utf8) ?? ""
        return model.components(separatedBy: "<object ").dropFirst().compactMap { text -> (String, TriangleMesh)? in
            guard let name = text.firstMatch(of: /name="([^"]*)"/)?.1 else { return nil }
            var positions: [SIMD3<Float>] = []
            for match in text.matches(of: /<vertex x="([^"]+)" y="([^"]+)" z="([^"]+)"\/>/) {
                positions.append(SIMD3(Float(match.1) ?? .nan, Float(match.2) ?? .nan, Float(match.3) ?? .nan))
            }
            var triangles: [UInt32] = []
            for match in text.matches(of: /<triangle v1="(\d+)" v2="(\d+)" v3="(\d+)"\/>/) {
                triangles += [UInt32(match.1) ?? 0, UInt32(match.2) ?? 0, UInt32(match.3) ?? 0]
            }
            return (String(name), TriangleMesh(positions: positions, triangles: triangles))
        }
    }

        var builder = GraphBuilder()
```
